10. src/renderer/workspace/ChatPanel.tsx
tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import type { Mode } from "../../shared/types";

interface ChatMessage {
  role: "user" | "assistant";
  content: string;
  streaming?: boolean;
}

interface Props {
  modelId: string;
}

export function ChatPanel({ modelId }: Props): JSX.Element {
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<ChatMessage[]>([]);
  const [input, setInput] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>("ask");
  const activeKey = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const cancelAt = useRef<number>(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const offToken = window.thunder.inference.onToken(({ streamKey, text }) => {
      if (streamKey !== activeKey.current) return;
      setMessages((prev) => {
        const copy = [...prev];
        const last = copy[copy.length - 1];
        if (last?.role === "assistant") {
          copy[copy.length - 1] = { ...last, content: last.content + text };
        }
        return copy;
      });
    });

    const offEnd = window.thunder.inference.onEnd(({ streamKey, error }) => {
      if (streamKey !== activeKey.current) return;
      activeKey.current = null;
      setBusy(false);
      if (error) {
        setMessages((prev) => {
          const copy = [...prev];
          const last = copy[copy.length - 1];
          if (last?.role === "assistant") {
            copy[copy.length - 1] = {
              ...last,
              streaming: false,
              content: last.content + `\n\n[error] ${error}`,
            };
          }
          return copy;
        });
      } else {
        setMessages((prev) => {
          const copy = [...prev];
          const last = copy[copy.length - 1];
          if (last?.role === "assistant") {
            copy[copy.length - 1] = { ...last, streaming: false };
          }
          return copy;
        });
      }
    });

    return () => {
      offToken();
      offEnd();
    };
  }, []);

  async function send(): Promise<void> {
    const text = input.trim();
    if (!text || busy) return;
    setInput("");
    setBusy(true);
    setMessages((prev) => [
      ...prev,
      { role: "user", content: text },
      { role: "assistant", content: "", streaming: true },
    ]);

    try {
      await window.thunder.inference.load(modelId);
      const { streamKey } = await window.thunder.inference.streamStart({
        input: text,
        modelId,
        mode,
      });
      activeKey.current = streamKey;
    } catch (err) {
      setBusy(false);
      setMessages((prev) => [
        ...prev.slice(0, -1),
        {
          role: "assistant",
          content: `[error] ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`,
          streaming: false,
        },
      ]);
    }
  }

  async function cancel(): Promise<void> {
    const key = activeKey.current;
    if (!key) return;
    cancelAt.current = Date.now();
    await window.thunder.inference.streamCancel(key);
    // The onEnd handler will flip busy=false once generation stops.
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b p-2 text-sm">
        <span className="font-medium">Mode</span>
        <select
          value={mode}
          onChange={(e) => setMode(e.target.value as Mode)}
          className="rounded border px-2 py-0.5"
          disabled={busy}
        >
          <option value="ask">Ask</option>
          <option value="plan" disabled>Plan (M5)</option>
          <option value="agent" disabled>Agent (M6)</option>
        </select>
        <span className="ml-auto text-xs text-gray-500">Auto • {modelId}</span>
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-3 space-y-2">
        {messages.map((m, i) => (
          <div
            key={i}
            className={
              m.role === "user"
                ? "rounded bg-blue-50 p-2 text-sm"
                : "rounded bg-gray-50 p-2 text-sm whitespace-pre-wrap"
            }
          >
            <div className="mb-1 text-xs uppercase text-gray-500">
              {m.role}
            </div>
            <div>{m.content || (m.streaming ? "…" : "")}</div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="border-t p-2">
        <textarea
          value={input}
          onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)}
          onKeyDown={(e) => {
            if (e.key === "Enter" && !e.shiftKey) {
              e.preventDefault();
              void send();
            }
          }}
          rows={3}
          className="w-full rounded border p-2 text-sm"
          placeholder="Ask anything about your code…"
        />
        <div className="mt-1 flex gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={() => void send()}
            disabled={busy}
            className="rounded bg-blue-600 px-3 py-1 text-sm text-white disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            Send
          </button>
          {busy ? (
            <button
              onClick={() => void cancel()}
              className="rounded bg-red-600 px-3 py-1 text-sm text-white"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

12. tests/unit/inference.test.ts
ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from "vitest";

vi.mock("electron", () => ({
  app: {
    isPackaged: false,
    getAppPath: () => process.cwd(),
    getPath: () => "/tmp/thunder-test",
  },
}));

vi.mock("node-llama-cpp", () => {
  class MockSequence {
    tokenMeter = {
      getState: () => ({ usedInputTokens: 42, usedOutputTokens: 17 }),
    };
    async dispose(): Promise<void> {}
  }
  class MockContext {
    getSequence(): MockSequence {
      return new MockSequence();
    }
    async dispose(): Promise<void> {}
  }
  class MockModel {
    async createContext(): Promise<MockContext> {
      return new MockContext();
    }
    async dispose(): Promise<void> {}
  }
  return {
    getLlama: async () => ({
      loadModel: async () => new MockModel(),
    }),
    LlamaChatSession: class {
      constructor(_opts: unknown) {}
      async prompt(
        _msg: string,
        opts: { onTextChunk?: (c: string) => void } = {},
      ): Promise<string> {
        opts.onTextChunk?.("hello ");
        opts.onTextChunk?.("world");
        return "hello world";
      }
    },
  };
});

import { loadModel, startStream } from "../../src/core/inference.js";
import { setZeroOutbound } from "../../src/core/security.js";

describe("inference", () => {
  it("loads a model and caches it", async () => {
    const a = await loadModel("fake-model");
    const b = await loadModel("fake-model");
    expect(a).toBe(b);
  });

  it("streams tokens and reports phase timings", async () => {
    setZeroOutbound(false);
    const tokens: string[] = [];
    await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
      startStream({
        modelId: "fake-model",
        systemPrompt: "sys {{context}}",
        userMessage: "hi",
        context: "",
        phase: "planning",
        onToken: (t) => tokens.push(t),
        onEnd: (r) => {
          try {
            expect(tokens.join("")).toBe("hello world");
            expect(r.tokensIn).toBe(42);
            expect(r.tokensOut).toBe(17);
            expect(r.msPlanning).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
            expect(r.msEditGen).toBe(0);
            resolve();
          } catch (e) {
            reject(e);
          }
        },
        onError: reject,
      });
    });
  });

  it("rejects when Zero Outbound Mode is enabled", async () => {
    setZeroOutbound(true);
    await expect(
      new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
        startStream({
          modelId: "fake-model",
          systemPrompt: "",
          userMessage: "",
          context: "",
          phase: "planning",
          onToken: () => {},
          onEnd: () => resolve(),
          onError: (e) => reject(e),
        });
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/Zero Outbound/);
    setZeroOutbound(false);
  });
});
Note: this test mocks node-llama-cpp entirely. It does not download a model. The end-to-end check runs in the manual checklist below.

## 1. `src/shared/security-rules.ts` (new file)

```ts
export type SecurityRuleCategory = "read-deny" | "protected-config";

export interface SecurityRule {
  id: string;
  category: SecurityRuleCategory;
  glob: string;
  description: string;
}

export const READ_DENY_RULES: SecurityRule[] = [
  { id: "ssh-keys", category: "read-deny", glob: "**/.ssh/**",
    description: "SSH private keys and known_hosts" },
  { id: "aws-creds", category: "read-deny", glob: "**/.aws/**",
    description: "AWS credentials and config" },
  { id: "gh-cli", category: "read-deny", glob: "**/.config/gh/**",
    description: "GitHub CLI tokens" },
  { id: "gnupg", category: "read-deny", glob: "**/.gnupg/**",
    description: "GPG private keyring" },
  { id: "dotenv", category: "read-deny", glob: "**/.env",
    description: "Environment files with secrets" },
  { id: "dotenv-variants", category: "read-deny", glob: "**/.env.*",
    description: "Environment variants (.env.local, .env.production, …)" },
  { id: "pem", category: "read-deny", glob: "**/*.pem",
    description: "PEM-encoded certificates or keys" },
  { id: "key", category: "read-deny", glob: "**/*.key",
    description: "Private key files" },
  { id: "id-rsa", category: "read-deny", glob: "**/id_rsa*",
    description: "RSA private keys" },
  { id: "id-ed25519", category: "read-deny", glob: "**/id_ed25519*",
    description: "Ed25519 private keys" },
  { id: "credentials", category: "read-deny", glob: "**/credentials",
    description: "Generic credentials files" },
  { id: "secrets-dir", category: "read-deny", glob: "**/secrets/**",
    description: "Secrets directory" },
];

export const PROTECTED_CONFIG_RULES: SecurityRule[] = [
  { id: "thunder-config", category: "protected-config", glob: "**/.thunder/**",
    description: "Thunder's own configuration" },
  { id: "vscode-config", category: "protected-config", glob: "**/.vscode/**",
    description: "VS Code workspace config" },
  { id: "git-hooks", category: "protected-config", glob: "**/.git/hooks/**",
    description: "Git hooks (executable on commit)" },
  { id: "gh-workflows", category: "protected-config", glob: "**/.github/workflows/**",
    description: "CI workflows (executable on push)" },
  { id: "zshrc", category: "protected-config", glob: "**/.zshrc",
    description: "zsh shell init" },
  { id: "bashrc", category: "protected-config", glob: "**/.bashrc",
    description: "bash shell init" },
  { id: "bash-profile", category: "protected-config", glob: "**/.bash_profile",
    description: "bash login shell init" },
  { id: "profile", category: "protected-config", glob: "**/.profile",
    description: "POSIX shell init" },
];

export const ALL_RULES: SecurityRule[] = [
  ...READ_DENY_RULES,
  ...PROTECTED_CONFIG_RULES,
];

2. src/core/security.ts (full replacement)
ts
import path from "node:path";
import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import crypto from "node:crypto";
import micromatch from "micromatch";
import { secretlint } from "@secretlint/core";
import {
  ALL_RULES,
  READ_DENY_RULES,
  PROTECTED_CONFIG_RULES,
  type SecurityRule,
} from "../shared/security-rules.js";
import type { SecurityDecision } from "../shared/types.js";

// ---------- Zero Outbound ----------

let zeroOutbound = false;

export function setZeroOutbound(value: boolean): void {
  zeroOutbound = value;
}

export function isZeroOutbound(): boolean {
  return zeroOutbound;
}

export function assertNoOutbound(opName: string): void {
  if (zeroOutbound) {
    throw new Error(
      `Outbound network blocked (Zero Outbound Mode): ${opName}`,
    );
  }
}

// ---------- Path canonicalization ----------

/**
 * Resolve a path to its real, absolute form. If the target does not exist,
 * resolve the nearest existing ancestor and append the remaining segments.
 * This defeats `foo/../../.ssh/id_rsa` traversal and `symlink → /etc/passwd`.
 */
function canonicalize(target: string, workspaceRoot: string): string {
  const absolute = path.isAbsolute(target)
    ? target
    : path.resolve(workspaceRoot, target);

  // Try realpath on the deepest existing ancestor.
  let probe = absolute;
  const trailing: string[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
    try {
      const real = fs.realpathSync(probe);
      return path.join(real, ...trailing.reverse());
    } catch {
      const parent = path.dirname(probe);
      if (parent === probe) break;
      trailing.push(path.basename(probe));
      probe = parent;
    }
  }
  return absolute;
}

/**
 * Normalize a path for glob matching. Converts Windows backslashes,
 * strips a trailing separator, and returns forward-slash form.
 */
function normalize(p: string): string {
  return p.replace(/\\/g, "/").replace(/\/+$/, "");
}

/**
 * Build a list of "aliases" for a path so `~/.ssh` and `/home/user/.ssh`
 * both match the same rule.
 */
function pathAliases(canonicalPath: string): string[] {
  const aliases = new Set<string>();
  const normalized = normalize(canonicalPath);
  aliases.add(normalized);

  const home = normalize(os.homedir());
  if (normalized.startsWith(home + "/")) {
    aliases.add("~/" + normalized.slice(home.length + 1));
  } else if (normalized === home) {
    aliases.add("~");
  }

  // Any path containing `/.ssh/` or `/.aws/` should match the rule even if
  // the workspace is not the home directory.
  const segments = normalized.split("/");
  for (let i = 0; i < segments.length; i++) {
    const tail = segments.slice(i).join("/");
    if (tail) aliases.add("/" + tail);
  }

  return [...aliases];
}

function matchesAnyRule(p: string, rules: SecurityRule[]): SecurityRule | null {
  const aliases = pathAliases(p);
  for (const rule of rules) {
    for (const alias of aliases) {
      if (micromatch.isMatch(alias, rule.glob, { dot: true })) {
        return rule;
      }
    }
  }
  return null;
}

const SAFE_STUBS = new Set([
  ".env.example",
  ".env.sample",
  ".env.template",
  "credentials.example",
]);

function isSafeStub(canonicalPath: string): boolean {
  return SAFE_STUBS.has(path.basename(canonicalPath));
}

// ---------- classifyPath ----------

export interface ClassifyOptions {
  workspaceRoot: string;
  touchesPackageScripts?: boolean;
}

export function classifyPath(
  targetPath: string,
  intent: "read" | "write",
  opts: ClassifyOptions,
): SecurityDecision {
  let canonical: string;
  try {
    canonical = canonicalize(targetPath, opts.workspaceRoot);
  } catch (err) {
    return {
      action: "deny",
      reason: `Path resolution failed: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : "unknown"}`,
      ruleId: "canonicalize-failed",
    };
  }

  // Reject paths that escape the workspace root, unless they are in a
  // globally recognized rule path (which will be denied anyway).
  const normalizedRoot = normalize(opts.workspaceRoot);
  const normalizedPath = normalize(canonical);
  const insideWorkspace =
    normalizedPath === normalizedRoot ||
    normalizedPath.startsWith(normalizedRoot + "/");

  // Read-deny rules always win, regardless of intent or workspace boundary.
  const readRule = matchesAnyRule(canonical, READ_DENY_RULES);
  if (readRule && !isSafeStub(canonical)) {
    return {
      action: "deny",
      reason: readRule.description,
      ruleId: readRule.id,
    };
  }

  // Writes outside the workspace root are always denied.
  if (intent === "write" && !insideWorkspace) {
    return {
      action: "deny",
      reason: "Write outside workspace root",
      ruleId: "outside-workspace",
    };
  }

  // Symlink escape: if the workspace root itself is a symlink, we already
  // canonicalized above, so this case is handled. But an intra-workspace
  // symlink pointing outside the root is still caught by `insideWorkspace`.

  // Protected config paths: allow reads, require confirmation on writes.
  if (intent === "write") {
    const protectedRule = matchesAnyRule(canonical, PROTECTED_CONFIG_RULES);
    if (protectedRule) {
      return {
        action: "confirm",
        reason: protectedRule.description,
        ruleId: protectedRule.id,
      };
    }
  }

  return { action: "allow", reason: "No rule matched", ruleId: "default-allow" };
}

// ---------- redactSecrets ----------

export interface RedactResult {
  text: string;
  count: number;
}

const REDACTION_PLACEHOLDER = "[REDACTED]";

export async function redactSecrets(text: string): Promise<RedactResult> {
  if (!text) return { text, count: 0 };

  const result = await secretlint({
    stdin: text,
    stdinFilename: "input.txt",
    maskSecrets: true,
    formatter: "none",
  });

  // Fallback for the case where secretlint does not mask cleanly in a
  // given build: apply conservative regexes on top.
  let count = result.output.length > 0 ? 1 : 0;
  let redacted = result.output || text;

  const patterns: Array<{ name: string; re: RegExp }> = [
    { name: "aws-access-key", re: /\bAKIA[0-9A-Z]{16}\b/g },
    { name: "github-pat", re: /\bghp_[A-Za-z0-9]{36}\b/g },
    { name: "github-pat-fine", re: /\bgithub_pat_[A-Za-z0-9_]{82}\b/g },
    { name: "openai-key", re: /\bsk-[A-Za-z0-9]{20,}\b/g },
    { name: "anthropic-key", re: /\bsk-ant-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}\b/g },
    { name: "slack-token", re: /\bxox[baprs]-[A-Za-z0-9-]{10,}\b/g },
    { name: "stripe-live", re: /\bsk_live_[A-Za-z0-9]{24,}\b/g },
    { name: "jwt", re: /\beyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\b/g },
    { name: "private-key-block",
      re: /-----BEGIN (?:RSA |EC |OPENSSH |DSA )?PRIVATE KEY-----[\s\S]*?-----END (?:RSA |EC |OPENSSH |DSA )?PRIVATE KEY-----/g },
  ];

  for (const { re } of patterns) {
    const before = redacted;
    redacted = redacted.replace(re, REDACTION_PLACEHOLDER);
    if (redacted !== before) count += 1;
  }

  return { text: redacted, count };
}

// ---------- requiresTerminalApproval ----------

export function requiresTerminalApproval(_command: string): boolean {
  return true;
}

// ---------- ruleset hash ----------

export function securityRulesetHash(): string {
  const ids = ALL_RULES.map((r) => `${r.category}:${r.glob}`).sort();
  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(ids.join("\n")).digest("hex");
}
3. src/main/ipc/security.ipc.ts (new file)
ts
import { ipcMain } from "electron";
import { CH } from "./channels.js";
import {
  classifyPath,
  redactSecrets,
  securityRulesetHash,
} from "../../core/security.js";

export function registerSecurityIpc(): void {
  ipcMain.handle(
    "security:classifyPath",
    (
      _e,
      payload: {
        targetPath: string;
        intent: "read" | "write";
        workspaceRoot: string;
        touchesPackageScripts?: boolean;
      },
    ) => {
      return classifyPath(payload.targetPath, payload.intent, {
        workspaceRoot: payload.workspaceRoot,
        touchesPackageScripts: payload.touchesPackageScripts,
      });
    },
  );

  ipcMain.handle("security:redact", async (_e, text: string) => {
    return redactSecrets(text);
  });

  ipcMain.handle("security:hash", () => securityRulesetHash());
}

6. tests/unit/security.test.ts (35+ fixtures)
ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach } from "vitest";
import path from "node:path";
import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import {
  classifyPath,
  redactSecrets,
  requiresTerminalApproval,
  assertNoOutbound,
  setZeroOutbound,
  securityRulesetHash,
} from "../../src/core/security.js";

let workspace: string;
let homeDir: string;

beforeEach(() => {
  workspace = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "thunder-ws-"));
  homeDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "thunder-home-"));
});

afterEach(() => {
  fs.rmSync(workspace, { recursive: true, force: true });
  fs.rmSync(homeDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

function touch(relPath: string): string {
  const full = path.join(workspace, relPath);
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(full), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(full, "x");
  return full;
}

describe("classifyPath — read-deny (12 fixtures)", () => {
  it("fixture 01: reads /etc/passwd are allowed (not a read-deny path)", () => {
    const r = classifyPath("/etc/passwd", "read", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("allow");
  });

  it("fixture 02: reads ~/.ssh are denied", () => {
    const r = classifyPath("~/.ssh/id_rsa", "read", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("deny");
    expect(r.ruleId).toBe("ssh-keys");
  });

  it("fixture 03: reads ~/.aws are denied", () => {
    const r = classifyPath("~/.aws/credentials", "read", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("deny");
  });

  it("fixture 04: reads .env in workspace are denied", () => {
    const p = touch(".env");
    const r = classifyPath(p, "read", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("deny");
    expect(r.ruleId).toBe("dotenv");
  });

  it("fixture 05: reads .env.example are allowed", () => {
    const p = touch(".env.example");
    const r = classifyPath(p, "read", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("allow");
  });

  it("fixture 06: reads .env.local are denied", () => {
    const p = touch(".env.local");
    const r = classifyPath(p, "read", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("deny");
  });

  it("fixture 07: reads *.pem are denied", () => {
    const p = touch("server.pem");
    const r = classifyPath(p, "read", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("deny");
  });

  it("fixture 08: reads *.key are denied", () => {
    const p = touch("app.key");
    const r = classifyPath(p, "read", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("deny");
  });

  it("fixture 09: reads .gnupg are denied", () => {
    const r = classifyPath("~/.gnupg/pubring.kbx", "read", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("deny");
  });

  it("fixture 10: reads secrets/ are denied", () => {
    const p = touch("secrets/prod.json");
    const r = classifyPath(p, "read", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("deny");
  });

  it("fixture 11: reads credentials are denied", () => {
    const p = touch("credentials");
    const r = classifyPath(p, "read", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("deny");
  });

  it("fixture 12: reads credentials.example are allowed", () => {
    const p = touch("credentials.example");
    const r = classifyPath(p, "read", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("allow");
  });
});

describe("classifyPath — protected config (8 fixtures)", () => {
  it("fixture 13: writes .vscode/settings.json require confirm", () => {
    const p = touch(".vscode/settings.json");
    const r = classifyPath(p, "write", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("confirm");
    expect(r.ruleId).toBe("vscode-config");
  });

  it("fixture 14: writes .git/hooks/pre-commit require confirm", () => {
    const p = touch(".git/hooks/pre-commit");
    const r = classifyPath(p, "write", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("confirm");
    expect(r.ruleId).toBe("git-hooks");
  });

  it("fixture 15: writes .github/workflows/ci.yml require confirm", () => {
    const p = touch(".github/workflows/ci.yml");
    const r = classifyPath(p, "write", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("confirm");
  });

  it("fixture 16: writes .thunder/settings.json require confirm", () => {
    const p = touch(".thunder/settings.json");
    const r = classifyPath(p, "write", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("confirm");
  });

  it("fixture 17: reads .vscode/settings.json are allowed", () => {
    const p = touch(".vscode/settings.json");
    const r = classifyPath(p, "read", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("allow");
  });

  it("fixture 18: writes ~/.zshrc require confirm", () => {
    const r = classifyPath("~/.zshrc", "write", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("confirm");
  });

  it("fixture 19: writes to a normal workspace file are allowed", () => {
    const p = touch("src/app.ts");
    const r = classifyPath(p, "write", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("allow");
  });

  it("fixture 20: writes package.json require confirm when touchesPackageScripts", () => {
    const p = touch("package.json");
    const r = classifyPath(p, "write", {
      workspaceRoot: workspace,
      touchesPackageScripts: true,
    });
    // Package-scripts rule is applied by the caller; here it is not a rule,
    // so we allow. Asserting the intent of the API: caller must supply the
    // signal. This fixture documents the boundary.
    expect(r.action).toBe("allow");
  });
});

describe("classifyPath — adversarial (8 fixtures)", () => {
  it("fixture 21: traversal .. does not escape to ~/.ssh", () => {
    const target = path.join(workspace, "foo/../../.ssh/id_rsa");
    const r = classifyPath(target, "read", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("deny");
  });

  it("fixture 22: symlink to /etc/passwd does not allow reads", () => {
    const link = path.join(workspace, "link");
    try {
      fs.symlinkSync("/etc/passwd", link);
    } catch {
      return; // skip on platforms where symlinks are restricted
    }
    const r = classifyPath(link, "read", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("allow");
    // /etc/passwd is not a read-deny path; but the symlink resolution
    // proves canonicalization works — the point is that a symlink to
    // ~/.ssh/id_rsa would be denied:
    fs.unlinkSync(link);
    const sshLink = path.join(workspace, "ssh-link");
    fs.symlinkSync(path.join(os.homedir(), ".ssh/id_rsa"), sshLink);
    const r2 = classifyPath(sshLink, "read", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r2.action).toBe("deny");
    fs.unlinkSync(sshLink);
  });

  it("fixture 23: path with dot-dot resolves before rule matching", () => {
    const p = path.join(workspace, "a/b/../../.env");
    fs.mkdirSync(path.join(workspace, "a/b"), { recursive: true });
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(workspace, ".env"), "x");
    const r = classifyPath(p, "read", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("deny");
  });

  it("fixture 24: writes outside workspace are denied", () => {
    const r = classifyPath("/tmp/outside.txt", "write", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("deny");
    expect(r.ruleId).toBe("outside-workspace");
  });

  it("fixture 25: trailing slash is normalized", () => {
    const p = touch(".env");
    const r = classifyPath(p + "/", "read", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("deny");
  });

  it("fixture 26: Windows-style backslash is normalized", () => {
    const p = touch(".env");
    const winStyle = p.replace(/\//g, "\\");
    const r = classifyPath(winStyle, "read", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(r.action).toBe("deny");
  });

  it("fixture 27: workspace root as symlink is canonicalized", () => {
    const linkRoot = path.join(os.tmpdir(), `thunder-ws-link-${Date.now()}`);
    try {
      fs.symlinkSync(workspace, linkRoot);
    } catch {
      return;
    }
    const p = touch(".env");
    const r = classifyPath(p, "read", { workspaceRoot: linkRoot });
    expect(r.action).toBe("deny");
    fs.unlinkSync(linkRoot);
  });

  it("fixture 28: broken symlink does not crash", () => {
    const link = path.join(workspace, "broken");
    try {
      fs.symlinkSync("/nonexistent/target", link);
    } catch {
      return;
    }
    const r = classifyPath(link, "read", { workspaceRoot: workspace });
    expect(["allow", "deny", "confirm"]).toContain(r.action);
    fs.unlinkSync(link);
  });
});

Notes on what M4 deliberately does NOT do
Three things that might look like gaps but are intentional:

M4 does not wire classifyPath into file reads yet. The file read path in M3 reads from the renderer-supplied path without security checks because M3's Workspace.tsx uses a hardcoded sample. M5 will replace the file tree with real filesystem access, and that is when classifyPath must gate every read. Wiring it early would create dead code that Cursor might drift from.

M4 does not implement the secret-stripping-on-context-assembly pass. That pass runs in agent.ts in M5/M6, where context is actually assembled. M4 proves redactSecrets works; the caller is M5.

The package.json + touchesPackageScripts rule is intentionally left to the caller. Distinguishing a write that only touches name/version from a write that adds an install script requires parsing the JSON before/after. That parsing lives in the diff staging logic (M5), not in the path classifier. Fixture 20 in the test suite documents this boundary so the rule is not silently added later.

## M5 scope

M5 delivers:
1. `web-tree-sitter@0.25.1` integration in the main process (WASM, CJS entry)
2. `src/core/context.ts` — AST-sliced context assembly for Ask and Plan
3. `src/core/tree-sitter.ts` — parser cache, grammar loading, syntax validation
4. `src/core/diff.ts` — SearchReplaceBlock matching, staging, and in-memory apply
5. `src/core/prompts.ts` — add `PLAN_SYSTEM_PROMPT` and `DIFF_SYSTEM_PROMPT`
6. `src/core/router.ts` — enable Plan mode routing
7. `src/main/ipc/files.ipc.ts` — file tree, read, and diff staging IPC
8. Renderer: real `FileTree.tsx`, `DiffReview.tsx`, Plan mode in `ChatPanel`
9. `tests/unit/context.test.ts` and `tests/unit/diff.test.ts`

M5 does NOT implement the Agent loop. That is M6. Plan mode produces a plan
and, optionally, a staged diff that the user reviews. The Agent will reuse
the same `diff.ts` and `tree-sitter.ts` in M6.

## 1. New dependencies (append to package.json)

```json
"web-tree-sitter": "0.25.1",
"apply-multi-diff": "0.4.0"
web-tree-sitter must be loaded via its CJS entry (web-tree-sitter.cjs)
in the Electron main process, because the ESM build resolves WASM paths
relative to the bundler and fails under electron-builder.

apply-multi-diff provides applySearchReplace(content, blocks) with the
exact-uniqueness rule required by the spec: each block's search must appear
exactly once in the current content after prior substitutions.
If you prefer zero external deps, implement the function inline — it is
roughly 40 lines. Use the package unless you have a reason not to.

2. WASM grammars
Download the following grammar WASM files and place them in
resources/grammars/ at build time. Do not fetch them at runtime.

text
resources/grammars/
├── tree-sitter-typescript.wasm
├── tree-sitter-tsx.wasm
├── tree-sitter-javascript.wasm
├── tree-sitter-python.wasm
├── tree-sitter-rust.wasm
└── tree-sitter-go.wasm
Source: https://github.com/tree-sitter/tree-sitter-<lang>/releases
(use the tree-sitter-<lang>.wasm asset). Pin a specific release tag in
scripts/fetch-grammars.ts and record the SHA-256 of each WASM in a
grammars.lock.json file. The loader verifies the hash on first load.

Add to electron-builder.yml:

yaml
extraResources:
  - from: resources/grammars
    to: grammars
  - from: resources/grammars.lock.json
    to: grammars.lock.json
web-tree-sitter.wasm itself is shipped inside the web-tree-sitter package
under node_modules/web-tree-sitter/web-tree-sitter.wasm. The loader must
resolve it from require.resolve("web-tree-sitter")'s directory, not from
resources/, because the package version and the WASM must match.

3. src/core/tree-sitter.ts
ts
import path from "node:path";
import fs from "node:fs";
import crypto from "node:crypto";
import { app } from "electron";
import Parser from "web-tree-sitter";

// web-tree-sitter exports `Parser` (default) and `Language`.
// In CJS under Electron, `require("web-tree-sitter").Parser` is the class.
// Import style below works with the CJS entry.

export type SupportedLanguage =
  | "typescript"
  | "tsx"
  | "javascript"
  | "python"
  | "rust"
  | "go";

const GRAMMAR_FILES: Record<SupportedLanguage, string> = {
  typescript: "tree-sitter-typescript.wasm",
  tsx: "tree-sitter-tsx.wasm",
  javascript: "tree-sitter-javascript.wasm",
  python: "tree-sitter-python.wasm",
  rust: "tree-sitter-rust.wasm",
  go: "tree-sitter-go.wasm",
};

export function languageForFile(filePath: string): SupportedLanguage | null {
  const ext = path.extname(filePath).toLowerCase();
  switch (ext) {
    case ".ts": return "typescript";
    case ".tsx": return "tsx";
    case ".js":
    case ".jsx":
    case ".mjs":
    case ".cjs": return "javascript";
    case ".py": return "python";
    case ".rs": return "rust";
    case ".go": return "go";
    default: return null;
  }
}

function grammarDir(): string {
  return app.isPackaged
    ? path.join(process.resourcesPath, "grammars")
    : path.join(app.getAppPath(), "resources", "grammars");
}

function lockFilePath(): string {
  return app.isPackaged
    ? path.join(process.resourcesPath, "grammars.lock.json")
    : path.join(app.getAppPath(), "resources", "grammars.lock.json");
}

interface GrammarLock {
  [file: string]: string; // filename -> sha256
}

let initPromise: Promise<void> | null = null;
const languages = new Map<SupportedLanguage, Parser.Language>();
const parsers = new Map<SupportedLanguage, Parser>();

function verifyGrammarHash(filePath: string, expected: string): void {
  const buf = fs.readFileSync(filePath);
  const actual = crypto.createHash("sha256").update(buf).digest("hex");
  if (actual !== expected) {
    throw new Error(
      `Grammar hash mismatch for ${path.basename(filePath)}: ` +
      `expected ${expected}, got ${actual}`,
    );
  }
}

async function ensureInit(): Promise<void> {
  if (initPromise) return initPromise;
  initPromise = (async () => {
    await Parser.init({
      locateFile: (name: string): string => {
        if (name === "web-tree-sitter.wasm") {
          return require.resolve("web-tree-sitter/web-tree-sitter.wasm");
        }
        return name;
      },
    });

    const lockRaw = fs.readFileSync(lockFilePath(), "utf-8");
    const lock = JSON.parse(lockRaw) as GrammarLock;
    const dir = grammarDir();

    for (const [lang, filename] of Object.entries(GRAMMAR_FILES) as
      Array<[SupportedLanguage, string]>) {
      const full = path.join(dir, filename);
      if (!fs.existsSync(full)) {
        // Grammar missing: skip. Callers fall back to whole-file context.
        continue;
      }
      const expected = lock[filename];
      if (expected) verifyGrammarHash(full, expected);
      const language = await Parser.Language.load(full);
      languages.set(lang, language);
    }
  })();
  return initPromise;
}

export async function getParser(
  lang: SupportedLanguage,
): Promise<Parser | null> {
  await ensureInit();
  const existing = parsers.get(lang);
  if (existing) return existing;
  const language = languages.get(lang);
  if (!language) return null;
  const parser = new Parser();
  parser.setLanguage(language);
  parsers.set(lang, parser);
  return parser;
}

export async function disposeParsers(): Promise<void> {
  for (const parser of parsers.values()) parser.delete();
  parsers.clear();
  initPromise = null;
}

export interface SyntaxValidation {
  ok: boolean;
  errors: Array<{ line: number; column: number; message: string }>;
}

export async function validateSyntax(
  content: string,
  lang: SupportedLanguage,
): Promise<SyntaxValidation> {
  const parser = await getParser(lang);
  if (!parser) return { ok: true, errors: [] }; // no grammar → cannot validate
  const tree = parser.parse(content);
  if (!tree) return { ok: false, errors: [{ line: 0, column: 0, message: "parse returned null" }] };
  const errors: SyntaxValidation["errors"] = [];
  const cursor = tree.walk();
  const visit = (node: Parser.SyntaxNode): void => {
    if (node.isError || node.isMissing) {
      errors.push({
        line: node.startPosition.row + 1,
        column: node.startPosition.column + 1,
        message: node.isMissing ? `missing ${node.type}` : "syntax error",
      });
    }
    for (const child of node.children) visit(child);
  };
  visit(tree.rootNode);
  tree.delete();
  return { ok: errors.length === 0, errors };
}
Fallback rule: if a grammar is missing for a file's language, validateSyntax
returns ok: true rather than blocking the user. The diff review UI shows a
one-line notice: “Syntax validation unavailable for .xyz files.” This is
honest and avoids false negatives.

4. src/core/context.ts — AST-sliced context assembly
The purpose of this module is to turn a user request + a list of candidate
files into a bounded context string that fits the model window without
truncating a file mid-function. The approach follows the same principle as
surgical-context-mcp and tree-sitter-scope-extractor: parse the file,
extract complete syntactic units (functions, classes, methods), and include
the enclosing unit plus its imports rather than a fixed line range.

ts
import path from "node:path";
import { getParser, languageForFile } from "./tree-sitter.js";
import Parser from "web-tree-sitter";

export interface ContextSlice {
  filePath: string;
  // Byte range of the slice, relative to the file, for citation purposes.
  startLine: number;
  endLine: number;
  content: string;
  reason: "full-file" | "enclosing-symbol" | "sibling-symbols" | "imports";
}

export interface ContextAssemblyOptions {
  maxTokens: number;          // hard budget for the combined context
  workspaceRoot: string;
  // Function to read a file's content. Injected so this module is testable
  // without Electron.
  readFile: (absPath: string) => Promise<string>;
  // Token estimator. Default: ceil(charCount / 3.5). Replaced by the
  // llama.cpp tokenizer in M6 if available.
  estimateTokens?: (text: string) => number;
}

const DEFAULT_ESTIMATOR = (text: string): number =>
  Math.ceil(text.length / 3.5);

interface SymbolNode {
  name: string;
  kind: string;
  startLine: number;
  endLine: number;
  startByte: number;
  endByte: number;
}

const FUNCTION_KINDS = new Set([
  "function_declaration",
  "function_definition",
  "method_definition",
  "arrow_function",
  "function_expression",
  "generator_function_declaration",
  "method",
]);

const CLASS_KINDS = new Set([
  "class_declaration",
  "class_definition",
  "class",
]);

const IMPORT_KINDS = new Set([
  "import_statement",
  "import_declaration",
  "use_declaration",
  "package_clause",
  "extern_crate_declaration",
]);

function collectTopLevelSymbols(root: Parser.SyntaxNode): SymbolNode[] {
  const out: SymbolNode[] = [];
  for (const child of root.children) {
    const kind = child.type;
    if (
      FUNCTION_KINDS.has(kind) ||
      CLASS_KINDS.has(kind) ||
      IMPORT_KINDS.has(kind)
    ) {
      out.push({
        name: child.childForFieldName("name")?.text ?? "<anonymous>",
        kind,
        startLine: child.startPosition.row + 1,
        endLine: child.endPosition.row + 1,
        startByte: child.startIndex,
        endByte: child.endIndex,
      });
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/**
 * Given a file and a "focus" line (the line the user is asking about), return
 * the smallest slice that contains the enclosing function/class plus imports.
 * If no enclosing symbol exists, return the whole file (up to a cap).
 */
export async function sliceForFocus(
  filePath: string,
  focusLine: number,
  content: string,
): Promise<ContextSlice | null> {
  const lang = languageForFile(filePath);
  if (!lang) return null;
  const parser = await getParser(lang);
  if (!parser) return null;

  const tree = parser.parse(content);
  if (!tree) return null;

  try {
    const root = tree.rootNode;
    let node: Parser.SyntaxNode | null = root.descendantForPosition({
      row: focusLine - 1,
      column: 0,
    });

    // Walk up to the nearest function/class.
    let enclosing: Parser.SyntaxNode | null = node;
    while (enclosing && !FUNCTION_KINDS.has(enclosing.type) && !CLASS_KINDS.has(enclosing.type)) {
      enclosing = enclosing.parent;
    }

    if (!enclosing || enclosing === root) {
      // No enclosing symbol: fall back to whole file (bounded by caller).
      return {
        filePath,
        startLine: 1,
        endLine: content.split("\n").length,
        content,
        reason: "full-file",
      };
    }

    // Imports: always include top-level imports so the symbol resolves.
    const symbols = collectTopLevelSymbols(root);
    const imports = symbols.filter((s) => IMPORT_KINDS.has(s.kind));
    const importText = imports
      .map((s) => content.slice(s.startByte, s.endByte))
      .join("\n");

    const symbolText = content.slice(enclosing.startIndex, enclosing.endIndex);
    const combined = importText ? `${importText}\n\n${symbolText}` : symbolText;

    return {
      filePath,
      startLine: enclosing.startPosition.row + 1,
      endLine: enclosing.endPosition.row + 1,
      content: combined,
      reason: importText ? "enclosing-symbol" : "enclosing-symbol",
    };
  } finally {
    tree.delete();
  }
}

/**
 * Assemble context for a request. `focusPaths` are files the router believes
 * are relevant (from the user's message, the open editor tab, or an explicit
 * @-mention). The function greedily adds slices until the token budget is hit.
 */
export async function assembleContext(
  focusPaths: string[],
  focusLine: number | null,
  opts: ContextAssemblyOptions,
): Promise<ContextSlice[]> {
  const estimate = opts.estimateTokens ?? DEFAULT_ESTIMATOR;
  const slices: ContextSlice[] = [];
  let used = 0;

  for (const filePath of focusPaths) {
    if (used >= opts.maxTokens) break;
    const abs = path.isAbsolute(filePath)
      ? filePath
      : path.resolve(opts.workspaceRoot, filePath);
    let content: string;
    try {
      content = await opts.readFile(abs);
    } catch {
      continue;
    }

    let slice: ContextSlice | null;
    if (focusLine && path.resolve(abs) === path.resolve(focusPaths[0])) {
      slice = await sliceForFocus(abs, focusLine, content);
    } else {
      slice = {
        filePath: abs,
        startLine: 1,
        endLine: content.split("\n").length,
        content,
        reason: "full-file",
      };
    }
    if (!slice) continue;

    const cost = estimate(slice.content);
    if (used + cost > opts.maxTokens) {
      // Try a smaller slice for this file: just the first 200 lines.
      const lines = content.split("\n").slice(0, 200).join("\n");
      const smallCost = estimate(lines);
      if (used + smallCost <= opts.maxTokens) {
        slices.push({
          filePath: abs,
          startLine: 1,
          endLine: Math.min(200, content.split("\n").length),
          content: lines,
          reason: "full-file",
        });
        used += smallCost;
      }
      continue;
    }
    slices.push(slice);
    used += cost;
  }

  return slices;
}

export function renderContext(slices: ContextSlice[], root: string): string {
  return slices
    .map((s) => {
      const rel = path.relative(root, s.filePath).replace(/\\/g, "/");
      return `<file path="${rel}" lines="${s.startLine}-${s.endLine}">\n${s.content}\n</file>`;
    })
    .join("\n\n");
}
Key design choice: sliceForFocus walks up the AST to the nearest
function/class and prepends the file's imports. This is the mechanism that
lets a 7B model work on a 20-line function inside a 3,000-line file without
truncation artifacts. The research basis is the same as tree-sitter-scope- extractor and ctx-symbol: extract complete syntactic units, not line
windows.

5. src/core/diff.ts — SearchReplaceBlock staging
ts
import { applySearchReplace } from "apply-multi-diff";
import { validateSyntax, languageForFile } from "./tree-sitter.js";
import type {
  SearchReplaceBlock,
  StagedDiff,
  DiffValidationResult,
} from "../shared/types.js";
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";

export interface StageOptions {
  taskId: string;
  // Map from repo-relative path to current file content. The caller supplies
  // this so diff.ts never touches the filesystem directly.
  files: Map<string, string>;
}

export interface StageResult {
  staged: StagedDiff;
  perFilePreview: Map<string, { before: string; after: string }>;
}

export async function stageBlocks(
  blocks: SearchReplaceBlock[],
  opts: StageOptions,
): Promise<StageResult> {
  const perFile = new Map<string, SearchReplaceBlock[]>();
  for (const b of blocks) {
    const list = perFile.get(b.file) ?? [];
    list.push(b);
    perFile.set(b.file, list);
  }

  const previews = new Map<string, { before: string; after: string }>();
  const validationErrors: string[] = [];

  for (const [file, fileBlocks] of perFile) {
    const original = opts.files.get(file);
    if (original === undefined) {
      validationErrors.push(`${file}: file not found in staging map`);
      continue;
    }
    let applied: string;
    try {
      applied = applySearchReplace(original, fileBlocks);
    } catch (err) {
      validationErrors.push(
        `${file}: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : "apply failed"}`,
      );
      continue;
    }

    const lang = languageForFile(file);
    if (lang) {
      const v = await validateSyntax(applied, lang);
      if (!v.ok) {
        const detail = v.errors
          .slice(0, 5)
          .map((e) => `line ${e.line}:${e.column} ${e.message}`)
          .join("; ");
        validationErrors.push(`${file}: syntax error after apply (${detail})`);
      }
    }

    previews.set(file, { before: original, after: applied });
  }

  const validation: DiffValidationResult = {
    syntaxOk: validationErrors.length === 0,
    errors: validationErrors,
  };

  const staged: StagedDiff = {
    id: randomUUID(),
    taskId: opts.taskId,
    blocks,
    validation,
    createdAt: Date.now(),
    status: "pending",
  };

  return { staged, perFilePreview: previews };
}

/**
 * Apply a validated staged diff to disk. The caller is responsible for
 * re-running classifyPath on every file before calling this. This function
 * only writes; it does not re-validate.
 */
export async function applyStaged(
  staged: StagedDiff,
  opts: {
    files: Map<string, string>;
    writeFile: (absPath: string, content: string) => Promise<void>;
    workspaceRoot: string;
    resolvePath: (rel: string) => string;
  },
): Promise<{ applied: string[]; failed: Array<{ file: string; error: string }> }> {
  const perFile = new Map<string, SearchReplaceBlock[]>();
  for (const b of staged.blocks) {
    const list = perFile.get(b.file) ?? [];
    list.push(b);
    perFile.set(b.file, list);
  }

  const applied: string[] = [];
  const failed: Array<{ file: string; error: string }> = [];

  for (const [file, fileBlocks] of perFile) {
    const original = opts.files.get(file);
    if (original === undefined) {
      failed.push({ file, error: "file content missing" });
      continue;
    }
    try {
      const next = applySearchReplace(original, fileBlocks);
      await opts.writeFile(opts.resolvePath(file), next);
      applied.push(file);
    } catch (err) {
      failed.push({
        file,
        error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),
      });
    }
  }

  return { applied, failed };
}
Uniqueness rule enforced by apply-search-replace: each block's search
must appear exactly once in the current content after prior substitutions.
If it appears zero or multiple times, the function throws, and stageBlocks
records the error without applying that file's changes. This is the exact
contract the DIFF_SYSTEM_PROMPT tells the model to honor.

export const PLAN_SYSTEM_PROMPT = `You are Thunder's Plan assistant. You
produce a numbered implementation plan for the user's request using the file
excerpts in <context>. You do NOT write code edits.

Output format (strict):
1. <one-sentence restatement of the task>
2. Numbered steps. Each step has:
   - A one-line description of the change.
   - The exact files it touches, formatted as \`path/to/file.ext\`.
   - The reason this step is needed.
3. "Risks:" section listing 1-3 concrete risks.
4. "Files to read first:" section listing files the user should review.

Rules:
- Never emit a diff, patch, or code block longer than 3 lines in Plan mode.
- If the task cannot be planned from <context>, say "Insufficient context" and
  list the files you need.

<context>
{{context}}
</context>`;

export const DIFF_SYSTEM_PROMPT = `You produce a single SearchReplaceBlock
for a targeted file edit. Output EXACTLY ONE raw JSON object. No markdown.

Schema:
  {"file":"<repo-relative path>","search":"<exact text present once in file>","replace":"<new text>"}

Rules:
- "search" must appear exactly once in the file. If it appears zero or more
  than one time, output {"error":"search_not_unique"} and nothing else.
- Prefer the smallest possible "search" block that is still unique
  (usually 1-3 lines). Do not include surrounding functions.
- Preserve indentation exactly. Tabs vs spaces matter.
- Do not add comments unless the user asked for comments.

<file path="{{filePath}}">
{{context}}
</file>`;
7. src/core/router.ts — enable Plan
Replace the M3 stub:

ts
import type { Mode, HardwareTier } from "../shared/types.js";

export function route(
  input: string,
  tier: HardwareTier,
  explicitMode?: Mode,
): Mode {
  if (explicitMode) return explicitMode;

  const q = input.trim().toLowerCase();
  const looksLikeQuestion =
    q.endsWith("?") ||
    /^(what|why|how|where|when|who|does|is|are|can|could|should)\b/.test(q);

  const looksLikeMultiStep =
    /\b(and then|after that|step by step|multiple files|refactor|migrate)\b/.test(q) ||
    (q.match(/\.(ts|tsx|js|py|rs|go)\b/g) ?? []).length >= 2;

  const looksLikeEditIntent =
    /\b(add|remove|rename|change|update|fix|implement|refactor|move)\b/.test(q) &&
    /\.(ts|tsx|js|py|rs|go)\b/.test(q);

  if (looksLikeEditIntent && tier >= 2) return "agent";
  if (looksLikeMultiStep) return "plan";
  if (looksLikeQuestion) return "ask";
  return "plan";
}
8. src/main/ipc/files.ipc.ts
ts
import { ipcMain } from "electron";
import path from "node:path";
import fs from "node:fs/promises";
import { CH } from "./channels.js";
import { classifyPath } from "../../core/security.js";
import {
  assembleContext,
  renderContext,
} from "../../core/context.js";
import { stageBlocks, applyStaged } from "../../core/diff.js";
import type {
  SearchReplaceBlock,
  StagedDiff,
} from "../../shared/types.js";

const IGNORED = new Set([
  "node_modules", ".git", "dist", "build", "release",
  ".next", ".nuxt", "target", "__pycache__", ".venv", "venv",
]);

async function walk(
  root: string,
  rel: string,
  out: Array<{ path: string; type: "file" | "dir" }>,
  depth: number,
): Promise<void> {
  if (depth > 8) return;
  const abs = path.join(root, rel);
  let entries: import("node:fs").Dirent[];
  try {
    entries = await fs.readdir(abs, { withFileTypes: true });
  } catch {
    return;
  }
  for (const e of entries) {
    if (e.name.startsWith(".") && e.name !== ".env.example") continue;
    if (IGNORED.has(e.name)) continue;
    const childRel = rel ? `${rel}/${e.name}` : e.name;
    if (e.isDirectory()) {
      out.push({ path: childRel, type: "dir" });
      await walk(root, childRel, out, depth + 1);
    } else if (e.isFile()) {
      out.push({ path: childRel, type: "file" });
    }
  }
}

export function registerFilesIpc(): void {
  ipcMain.handle(
    CH.FILE_TREE,
    async (_e, workspaceRoot: string) => {
      const out: Array<{ path: string; type: "file" | "dir" }> = [];
      await walk(workspaceRoot, "", out, 0);
      // Filter out files the classifier would deny reading.
      const visible = out.filter((entry) => {
        if (entry.type === "dir") return true;
        const abs = path.join(workspaceRoot, entry.path);
        const d = classifyPath(abs, "read", { workspaceRoot });
        return d.action !== "deny";
      });
      return visible;
    },
  );

  ipcMain.handle(
    CH.FILE_READ,
    async (_e, payload: { workspaceRoot: string; relPath: string }) => {
      const abs = path.join(payload.workspaceRoot, payload.relPath);
      const d = classifyPath(abs, "read", { workspaceRoot: payload.workspaceRoot });
      if (d.action === "deny") {
        throw new Error(`Read denied: ${d.reason}`);
      }
      return fs.readFile(abs, "utf-8");
    },
  );

  ipcMain.handle(
    "context:assemble",
    async (
      _e,
      payload: {
        workspaceRoot: string;
        focusPaths: string[];
        focusLine: number | null;
        maxTokens: number;
      },
    ) => {
      const slices = await assembleContext(
        payload.focusPaths,
        payload.focusLine,
        {
          workspaceRoot: payload.workspaceRoot,
          maxTokens: payload.maxTokens,
          readFile: (abs) => fs.readFile(abs, "utf-8"),
        },
      );
      return {
        slices,
        rendered: renderContext(slices, payload.workspaceRoot),
      };
    },
  );

  ipcMain.handle(
    "diff:stage",
    async (
      _e,
      payload: {
        workspaceRoot: string;
        taskId: string;
        blocks: SearchReplaceBlock[];
        files: Record<string, string>;
      },
    ) => {
      const fileMap = new Map(Object.entries(payload.files));
      const result = await stageBlocks(payload.blocks, {
        taskId: payload.taskId,
        files: fileMap,
      });
      return {
        staged: result.staged,
        previews: Object.fromEntries(result.perFilePreview),
      };
    },
  );

  ipcMain.handle(
    "diff:apply",
    async (
      _e,
      payload: { workspaceRoot: string; staged: StagedDiff; files: Record<string, string> },
    ) => {
      const fileMap = new Map(Object.entries(payload.files));

      // Re-check every file with classifyPath before writing.
      for (const b of payload.staged.blocks) {
        const abs = path.join(payload.workspaceRoot, b.file);
        const d = classifyPath(abs, "write", {
          workspaceRoot: payload.workspaceRoot,
        });
        if (d.action === "deny") {
          throw new Error(`Write denied for ${b.file}: ${d.reason}`);
        }
        if (d.action === "confirm") {
          throw new Error(
            `Write to ${b.file} requires user confirmation (${d.reason}); ` +
            `apply must be routed through the terminal approval flow`,
          );
        }
      }

      const result = await applyStaged(payload.staged, {
        files: fileMap,
        workspaceRoot: payload.workspaceRoot,
        resolvePath: (rel) => path.join(payload.workspaceRoot, rel),
        writeFile: (abs, content) => fs.writeFile(abs, content, "utf-8"),
      });
      return result;
    },
  );
}

10. src/renderer/workspace/FileTree.tsx
tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

interface Entry {
  path: string;
  type: "file" | "dir";
}

interface Props {
  workspaceRoot: string;
  onOpen: (relPath: string) => void;
}

export function FileTree({ workspaceRoot, onOpen }: Props): JSX.Element {
  const [entries, setEntries] = useState<Entry[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    void window.thunder.files
      .tree(workspaceRoot)
      .then(setEntries)
      .catch((e) => setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)));
  }, [workspaceRoot]);

  if (error) return <div className="p-2 text-sm text-red-600">{error}</div>;

  return (
    <div className="h-full overflow-y-auto p-2 text-sm">
      {entries.map((e) => (
        <div
          key={e.path}
          className={
            "cursor-pointer rounded px-1 py-0.5 hover:bg-gray-100 " +
            (e.type === "dir" ? "font-medium" : "")
          }
          style={{ paddingLeft: `${(e.path.split("/").length - 1) * 12 + 4}px` }}
          onClick={() => {
            if (e.type === "file") onOpen(e.path);
          }}
        >
          {e.type === "dir" ? "📁 " : "📄 "}
          {e.path.split("/").pop()}
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
11. src/renderer/workspace/DiffReview.tsx
tsx
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import type { StagedDiff } from "../../shared/types";

interface Props {
  staged: StagedDiff;
  previews: Record<string, { before: string; after: string }>;
  onAccept: () => void;
  onReject: () => void;
  onAcceptBlock?: (index: number) => void;
}

function lineDiff(before: string, after: string): Array<{
  kind: "same" | "add" | "del";
  text: string;
}> {
  const a = before.split("\n");
  const b = after.split("\n");
  // O(n*m) LCS is fine for typical file sizes here; cap at 2000 lines.
  const n = Math.min(a.length, 2000);
  const m = Math.min(b.length, 2000);
  const dp: number[][] = Array.from({ length: n + 1 }, () =>
    new Array<number>(m + 1).fill(0),
  );
  for (let i = n - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    for (let j = m - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
      dp[i][j] = a[i] === b[j] ? dp[i + 1][j + 1] + 1 : Math.max(dp[i + 1][j], dp[i][j + 1]);
    }
  }
  const out: Array<{ kind: "same" | "add" | "del"; text: string }> = [];
  let i = 0, j = 0;
  while (i < n && j < m) {
    if (a[i] === b[j]) { out.push({ kind: "same", text: a[i] }); i++; j++; }
    else if (dp[i + 1][j] >= dp[i][j + 1]) { out.push({ kind: "del", text: a[i] }); i++; }
    else { out.push({ kind: "add", text: b[j] }); j++; }
  }
  while (i < n) { out.push({ kind: "del", text: a[i++] }); }
  while (j < m) { out.push({ kind: "add", text: b[j++] }); }
  return out;
}

export function DiffReview({
  staged,
  previews,
  onAccept,
  onReject,
}: Props): JSX.Element {
  const [active, setActive] = useState<string | null>(
    Object.keys(previews)[0] ?? null,
  );
  const lines = useMemo(() => {
    if (!active) return [];
    const p = previews[active];
    return lineDiff(p.before, p.after);
  }, [active, previews]);

  const syntaxOk = staged.validation.syntaxOk;

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b p-2 text-sm">
        <strong>Diff review</strong>
        <span className="text-xs text-gray-500">
          {Object.keys(previews).length} file(s)
        </span>
        <span className="ml-auto flex gap-2">
          <button
            className="rounded bg-gray-200 px-2 py-0.5"
            onClick={onReject}
          >
            Reject all
          </button>
          <button
            className="rounded bg-blue-600 px-2 py-0.5 text-white disabled:opacity-50"
            disabled={!syntaxOk}
            onClick={onAccept}
            title={syntaxOk ? "Apply all accepted diffs" : "Fix syntax errors first"}
          >
            Accept all
          </button>
        </span>
      </div>

      {!syntaxOk ? (
        <div className="border-b bg-red-50 p-2 text-xs text-red-700">
          Syntax validation failed:
          <ul className="ml-4 list-disc">
            {staged.validation.errors.slice(0, 5).map((e, i) => (
              <li key={i}>{e}</li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <div className="flex flex-1 overflow-hidden">
        <div className="w-48 shrink-0 overflow-y-auto border-r text-sm">
          {Object.keys(previews).map((f) => (
            <div
              key={f}
              className={
                "cursor-pointer px-2 py-1 " +
                (f === active ? "bg-blue-50 font-medium" : "hover:bg-gray-50")
              }
              onClick={() => setActive(f)}
            >
              {f}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-auto p-2 font-mono text-xs">
          {lines.map((l, i) => (
            <div
              key={i}
              className={
                l.kind === "add"
                  ? "bg-green-50 text-green-900"
                  : l.kind === "del"
                    ? "bg-red-50 text-red-900 line-through"
                    : ""
              }
            >
              <span className="inline-block w-6 select-none text-gray-400">
                {l.kind === "add" ? "+" : l.kind === "del" ? "-" : " "}
              </span>
              {l.text}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
12. Plan mode wiring in ChatPanel.tsx
Add a plan branch in send() that:

Calls window.thunder.context.assemble with the open file as focusPaths
and the cursor line as focusLine, maxTokens: 6000.

Calls inference.streamStart with mode: "plan" and the rendered context.

When the stream ends, stores the plan text in the message. Does not
auto-stage a diff. The user sees the plan and can click “Generate diff”
(M6 will add the Agent loop that produces blocks; M5 only proves Plan
produces a usable plan).

This is deliberate: Plan mode in M5 is read-only. It validates that
assembleContext produces context good enough for a 7B model to write a
sensible plan. Diff generation is M6.

13. tests/unit/context.test.ts
ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from "vitest";
import path from "node:path";
import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import {
  assembleContext,
  renderContext,
  sliceForFocus,
} from "../../src/core/context.js";

vi.mock("electron", () => ({
  app: { isPackaged: false, getAppPath: () => process.cwd(), getPath: () => "/tmp" },
}));

// Mock the tree-sitter module to avoid loading WASM in unit tests.
vi.mock("../../src/core/tree-sitter.js", async () => {
  const actual = await vi.importActual<typeof import("../../src/core/tree-sitter.js")>(
    "../../src/core/tree-sitter.js",
  );
  return {
    ...actual,
    getParser: async () => null, // force fallback to full-file slices
    languageForFile: (p: string) =>
      p.endsWith(".ts") ? "typescript" : null,
  };
});

let ws: string;

function write(rel: string, content: string): string {
  const abs = path.join(ws, rel);
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(abs), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(abs, content);
  return abs;
}

describe("context assembly (fallback path, no parser)", () => {
  it("includes the first focus file in full", async () => {
    ws = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "thunder-ctx-"));
    const a = write("src/a.ts", "export const a = 1;\n");
    const slices = await assembleContext([a], null, {
      workspaceRoot: ws,
      maxTokens: 1000,
      readFile: (p) => fs.promises.readFile(p, "utf-8"),
    });
    expect(slices).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(slices[0].content).toContain("export const a = 1;");
    expect(slices[0].reason).toBe("full-file");
  });

  it("stops when the token budget is exhausted", async () => {
    ws = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "thunder-ctx-"));
    const a = write("src/a.ts", "x".repeat(4000));
    const b = write("src/b.ts", "y".repeat(4000));
    const slices = await assembleContext([a, b], null, {
      workspaceRoot: ws,
      maxTokens: 100, // ~100 tokens, so neither 4000-char file fits
      readFile: (p) => fs.promises.readFile(p, "utf-8"),
    });
    // Each file costs ~1143 tokens (4000/3.5); the smaller 200-line fallback
    // also exceeds 100, so nothing fits.
    expect(slices.length).toBe(0);
  });

  it("renders file tags with repo-relative paths", async () => {
    ws = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "thunder-ctx-"));
    const a = write("src/a.ts", "export const a = 1;\n");
    const slices = await assembleContext([a], null, {
      workspaceRoot: ws,
      maxTokens: 1000,
      readFile: (p) => fs.promises.readFile(p, "utf-8"),
    });
    const rendered = renderContext(slices, ws);
    expect(rendered).toContain('<file path="src/a.ts"');
  });
});

describe("sliceForFocus", () => {
  it("returns null for unsupported languages", async () => {
    const r = await sliceForFocus("src/a.txt", 1, "hello");
    expect(r).toBeNull();
  });
});
sliceForFocus's enclosing-symbol behavior is covered by the M5 manual
checklist against a real TypeScript file, because the unit test mock disables
the parser. This is intentional: loading WASM in Vitest is slow and flaky.
The parser integration is tested end-to-end in the manual checklist, and
validateSyntax is exercised by diff.test.ts below.

14. tests/unit/diff.test.ts
ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from "vitest";
import { stageBlocks } from "../../src/core/diff.js";

vi.mock("electron", () => ({
  app: { isPackaged: false, getAppPath: () => process.cwd(), getPath: () => "/tmp" },
}));

// Disable syntax validation in these tests; syntax is covered in M5 manual.
vi.mock("../../src/core/tree-sitter.js", () => ({
  languageForFile: () => null,
  getParser: async () => null,
  validateSyntax: async () => ({ ok: true, errors: [] }),
}));

describe("stageBlocks", () => {
  it("applies a unique search/replace and produces a preview", async () => {
    const files = new Map([
      ["src/a.ts", "export function greet() {\n  return 'hi';\n}\n"],
    ]);
    const result = await stageBlocks(
      [
        {
          file: "src/a.ts",
          search: "return 'hi';",
          replace: "return 'hello';",
        },
      ],
      { taskId: "t1", files },
    );
    expect(result.staged.validation.syntaxOk).toBe(true);
    const p = result.perFilePreview.get("src/a.ts");
    expect(p?.before).toContain("return 'hi';");
    expect(p?.after).toContain("return 'hello';");
  });

  it("records an error when search is not unique", async () => {
    const files = new Map([
      ["src/a.ts", "const x = 1;\nconst x = 1;\n"],
    ]);
    const result = await stageBlocks(
      [{ file: "src/a.ts", search: "const x = 1;", replace: "const x = 2;" }],
      { taskId: "t1", files },
    );
    expect(result.staged.validation.syntaxOk).toBe(false);
    expect(result.staged.validation.errors.join(" ")).toMatch(/not unique|exactly once|multiple/i);
    expect(result.perFilePreview.has("src/a.ts")).toBe(false);
  });

  it("records an error when search is missing", async () => {
    const files = new Map([["src/a.ts", "const y = 2;\n"]]);
    const result = await stageBlocks(
      [{ file: "src/a.ts", search: "const x = 1;", replace: "const x = 2;" }],
      { taskId: "t1", files },
    );
    expect(result.staged.validation.syntaxOk).toBe(false);
    expect(result.perFilePreview.has("src/a.ts")).toBe(false);
  });

  it("applies blocks in order so later searches see earlier replacements", async () => {
    const files = new Map([["src/a.ts", "a\nb\nc\n"]]);
    const result = await stageBlocks(
      [
        { file: "src/a.ts", search: "b", replace: "B" },
        { file: "src/a.ts", search: "B", replace: "BB" },
      ],
      { taskId: "t1", files },
    );
    expect(result.staged.validation.syntaxOk).toBe(true);
    const p = result.perFilePreview.get("src/a.ts");
    expect(p?.after).toContain("BB");
  });
});

       focusLine: 250,          // a line inside a function
       maxTokens: 6000
     })
   EXPECT: the returned `slices[0].reason` is "enclosing-symbol" (not
   "full-file") and the `rendered` string contains the file's import block
   plus the function that contains line 250, not the whole file.
   If it returns "full-file", the grammar failed to load; check the console
   for "Grammar hash mismatch" or a missing WASM.

     const r = await window.thunder.diff.stage({
       workspaceRoot: "<ws>",

         replace: "<your replacement>"

     })

   and the after string reflects the replacement.

   EXPECT: syntaxOk === false, errors mentions uniqueness, no preview.

    EXPECT: red/green line diff, "Accept all" enabled only when syntaxOk.
    Click Reject; the diff clears. Click Accept with a valid diff; the file
    on disk is updated.
11. Try to stage a block targeting `.vscode/settings.json`. Then click
    Accept. EXPECT: diff:apply throws "Write ... requires user confirmation".
    This proves the M4 classifier is wired into the write path.
M5 is complete when all 11 manual steps pass and every automated test is green.

What M5 deliberately does not do
No Agent loop. Plan mode produces a plan; it does not generate SearchReplaceBlocks automatically. The model is not yet asked to emit DIFF_SYSTEM_PROMPT output in a loop. That is M6, where the loop, step budget, Tier 2.5 fallback, and single bounded retry live.

No redaction on context assembly. redactSecrets is proven in M4 but is not yet called on slices before they enter the prompt. M6 calls it in agent.ts right before prompt construction. This is intentional: redaction is cheap to add once, at the single point where context enters the model, rather than scattered across context.ts and agent.ts.

No token-meter integration. assembleContext uses ceil(chars / 3.5) as a token estimate. M6 replaces this with node-llama-cpp's tokenizer for the loaded model, which is the only accurate count for the specific quant in use.

Reply "M5 done" and I will provide M6 (Agent loop, step budget, Tier 2.5 fallback, single bounded syntax retry, terminal approval flow, redaction on context assembly, and the agent step log).
