# THUNDER — Continuation Spec (M6)
# Paste this AFTER M5. This spec is M6 only:
# the Agent loop, step budget, Tier 2.5 fallback, single bounded syntax retry,
# terminal approval flow, redaction on context assembly, grammar-constrained
# JSON tool calls, and the AgentStep log.
# Do not begin M6 until the M5 acceptance checklist is green.

## M6 scope

M6 delivers:
1. `src/core/agent.ts` — the bounded Agent loop with grammar-constrained JSON
   tool calls, step budget, Tier 2.5 fallback, and single bounded retry
2. `src/core/prompts.ts` — finalize `AGENT_SYSTEM_PROMPT` (already drafted in
   section 13 of the main spec; M6 wires it into the loop)
3. `src/main/ipc/agent.ipc.ts` — Agent start / abort / step stream / terminal
   response IPC
4. `src/main/ipc/terminal.ipc.ts` — terminal approval request / response
5. Renderer: `TerminalApproval.tsx` dialog, Agent mode wiring in `ChatPanel`,
   "Next Step" cards for Tier 2.5 fallback
6. `tests/unit/agent.test.ts`

M6 does NOT implement the Pro tier, entitlements, or packaging. Those are M7–M9.

## 1. Grammar-constrained JSON tool calls

`node-llama-cpp` exposes `llama.createGrammarForJsonSchema(schema)`, which
compiles a JSON Schema into a GBNF grammar and constrains generation at the
token level[reference:0]. M6 uses this so the Agent cannot emit malformed JSON,
which is the single biggest failure mode for 7B models in tool-calling loops.

**Critical constraint from the research literature:** llama.cpp's GBNF parser
has a repetition safeguard of 2000. A valid `maxLength: 4000` in a JSON Schema
compiles to `char{0,4000}` and fails *before inference starts* with
“number of repetitions exceeds sane defaults”[reference:1]. Thunder's Agent
schema must therefore **avoid `maxLength` and `minLength` entirely**, and rely
on post-parse validation for string length. This is not optional; it is a
known pre-inference failure path.

### `src/core/agent-schema.ts` (new file)

```ts
import { z } from "zod";

/**
 * JSON Schema for a single Agent turn. Do NOT add maxLength / minLength —
 * llama.cpp's GBNF parser rejects repetition bounds above 2000 and the whole
 * sampler fails before the model generates a token.
 */
export const AGENT_TURN_JSON_SCHEMA = {
  type: "object",
  properties: {
    kind: {
      enum: ["read", "search_replace", "terminal", "finish"],
    },
    path: { type: "string" },
    blocks: {
      type: "array",
      items: {
        type: "object",
        properties: {
          file: { type: "string" },
          search: { type: "string" },
          replace: { type: "string" },
        },
        required: ["file", "search", "replace"],
        additionalProperties: false,
      },
    },
    command: { type: "string" },
    cwd: { type: "string" },
    reason: { type: "string" },
    summary: { type: "string" },
  },
  required: ["kind"],
  additionalProperties: false,
} as const;

/**
 * Runtime validation after grammar-constrained generation. Catches semantic
 * errors the grammar cannot express (empty strings, path traversal attempts,
 * block count limits).
 */
export const AgentTurnSchema = z.discriminatedUnion("kind", [
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("read"), path: z.string().min(1) }),
  z.object({
    kind: z.literal("search_replace"),
    blocks: z
      .array(
        z.object({
          file: z.string().min(1),
          search: z.string().min(1),
          replace: z.string(),
        }),
      )
      .min(1)
      .max(3),
  }),
  z.object({
    kind: z.literal("terminal"),
    command: z.string().min(1),
    cwd: z.string().min(1),
    reason: z.string().min(1),
  }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("finish"), summary: z.string().min(1) }),
]);

export type AgentTurn = z.infer<typeof AgentTurnSchema>;
2. src/core/agent.ts
ts
import path from "node:path";
import crypto from "node:crypto";
import {
  getLlama,
  LlamaChatSession,
  type Llama,
  type LlamaModel,
  type LlamaContext,
} from "node-llama-cpp";
import { AGENT_TURN_JSON_SCHEMA, AgentTurnSchema, type AgentTurn } from "./agent-schema.js";
import { AGENT_SYSTEM_PROMPT } from "./prompts.js";
import { redactSecrets } from "./security.js";
import { stageBlocks } from "./diff.js";
import { validateSyntax, languageForFile } from "./tree-sitter.js";
import { capabilitiesFor } from "./hardware.js";
import type {
  AgentStep,
  HardwareTier,
  SearchReplaceBlock,
  StagedDiff,
} from "../shared/types.js";

export interface AgentFileIO {
  readFile: (absPath: string) => Promise<string>;
  listDir: (absPath: string) => Promise<Array<{ name: string; isDir: boolean }>>;
  writeFile: (absPath: string, content: string) => Promise<void>;
}

export interface AgentCallbacks {
  onStep: (step: AgentStep) => void;
  onToken: (streamKey: string, text: string) => void;
  onDiff: (staged: StagedDiff, previews: Map<string, { before: string; after: string }>) => void;
  onTerminalRequest: (req: {
    requestId: string;
    command: string;
    cwd: string;
    reason: string;
  }) => Promise<"allow" | "deny">;
  onFallback: (cards: Array<{ file: string; instruction: string }>) => void;
  onFinish: (summary: string, steps: AgentStep[]) => void;
  onError: (err: Error) => void;
}

export interface AgentRunOptions {
  taskId: string;
  userMessage: string;
  tier: HardwareTier;
  workspaceRoot: string;
  modelId: string;
  openFilePath: string | null;
  openFileLine: number | null;
  io: AgentFileIO;
  callbacks: AgentCallbacks;
  abortSignal: AbortSignal;
}

let llamaPromise: Promise<Llama> | null = null;
async function llamaInstance(): Promise<Llama> {
  if (!llamaPromise) llamaPromise = getLlama({ gpu: "auto" });
  return llamaPromise;
}

const modelCache = new Map<string, { model: LlamaModel; context: LlamaContext }>();

async function loadCached(modelId: string, modelsDir: string): Promise<{ model: LlamaModel; context: LlamaContext }> {
  const hit = modelCache.get(modelId);
  if (hit) return hit;
  const llama = await llamaInstance();
  const model = await llama.loadModel({ modelPath: path.join(modelsDir, `${modelId}.gguf`) });
  const context = await model.createContext({ contextSize: 8192 });
  const entry = { model, context };
  modelCache.set(modelId, entry);
  return entry;
}

interface BuiltContext {
  contextString: string;
  redactionCount: number;
}

async function buildContext(
  opts: AgentRunOptions,
  history: string[],
): Promise<BuiltContext> {
  // M6 keeps context assembly deliberately simple: the open file plus the
  // running scratchpad. AST-sliced context from M5 is used when openFilePath
  // is set; otherwise the Agent works from the user message alone.
  let base = "";
  if (opts.openFilePath) {
    try {
      const content = await opts.io.readFile(path.join(opts.workspaceRoot, opts.openFilePath));
      base = `<file path="${opts.openFilePath}">\n${content}\n</file>`;
    } catch {
      // File unreadable; continue without it.
    }
  }
  const scratch = history.length > 0 ? `\n<scratchpad>\n${history.join("\n")}\n</scratchpad>` : "";
  const raw = `${base}${scratch}`;
  const redacted = await redactSecrets(raw);
  return { contextString: redacted.text, redactionCount: redacted.count };
}

function extractJson(text: string): string | null {
  // Grammar-constrained generation should already emit pure JSON, but some
  // model/template pairs wrap it in whitespace or a stray code fence. Strip
  // both before parsing.
  const trimmed = text.trim();
  if (trimmed.startsWith("{") && trimmed.endsWith("}")) return trimmed;
  const fence = trimmed.match(/```(?:json)?\s*([\s\S]*?)```/);
  if (fence) return fence[1].trim();
  const first = trimmed.indexOf("{");
  const last = trimmed.lastIndexOf("}");
  if (first >= 0 && last > first) return trimmed.slice(first, last + 1);
  return null;
}

export async function runAgent(opts: AgentRunOptions): Promise<void> {
  const caps = capabilitiesFor(opts.tier);
  if (!caps.modes.includes("agent")) {
    opts.callbacks.onError(new Error(`Agent mode unavailable on tier ${opts.tier}`));
    return;
  }

  const maxSteps = caps.maxFilesPerAgentRun * 4;
  const stepTimeoutMs = caps.agentStepTimeoutMs;
  const steps: AgentStep[] = [];
  const scratchpad: string[] = [];
  const seenReads = new Set<string>();

  let model: LlamaModel;
  let context: LlamaContext;
  try {
    const entry = await loadCached(opts.modelId, path.join(opts.workspaceRoot, "..", "models"));
    model = entry.model;
    context = entry.context;
  } catch (err) {
    opts.callbacks.onError(err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err)));
    return;
  }

  const grammar = await (await llamaInstance()).createGrammarForJsonSchema(AGENT_TURN_JSON_SCHEMA);

  let accumulatedBlocks: SearchReplaceBlock[] = [];
  let accumulatedFiles: Map<string, string> = new Map();
  let jsonRetries = 0;
  let syntaxRetries = 0;
  let fallbackTriggered = false;

  for (let stepIndex = 0; stepIndex < maxSteps; stepIndex++) {
    if (opts.abortSignal.aborted) {
      opts.callbacks.onFinish("Aborted by user", steps);
      return;
    }

    const stepStart = Date.now();
    const phase = accumulatedBlocks.length === 0 ? "planning" : "edit-gen";
    const built = await buildContext(opts, scratchpad);

    const sequence = context.getSequence();
    let rawText = "";
    let tokensIn = 0;
    let tokensOut = 0;
    let status: AgentStep["status"] = "ok";
    let parsed: AgentTurn | null = null;

    try {
      const session = new LlamaChatSession({
        contextSequence: sequence,
        systemPrompt: AGENT_SYSTEM_PROMPT.replace("{{context}}", built.contextString),
      });

      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        // Step timeout: abort the sequence so the loop can fall back cleanly.
        try { void sequence.dispose(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
      }, stepTimeoutMs);

      try {
        rawText = await session.prompt(opts.userMessage, {
          grammar,
          signal: opts.abortSignal,
          maxTokens: 512,
          temperature: 0.2,
          onTextChunk: (chunk) => {
            opts.callbacks.onToken(opts.taskId, chunk);
          },
        });
      } finally {
        clearTimeout(timer);
      }

      const meter = sequence.tokenMeter.getState();
      tokensIn = meter.usedInputTokens ?? 0;
      tokensOut = meter.usedOutputTokens ?? 0;

      const json = extractJson(rawText);
      if (!json) {
        status = "retried";
        jsonRetries += 1;
        if (jsonRetries > 1) {
          throw new Error("Model failed to emit JSON twice; aborting");
        }
        scratchpad.push(`Previous turn was not valid JSON. Emit exactly one raw JSON object.`);
        steps.push({
          index: stepIndex,
          kind: "plan",
          payload: { raw: rawText.slice(0, 200) },
          startedAt: stepStart,
          endedAt: Date.now(),
          phase,
          tokensIn,
          tokensOut,
          status,
        });
        opts.callbacks.onStep(steps[steps.length - 1]);
        continue;
      }

      const candidate = JSON.parse(json) as unknown;
      const validated = AgentTurnSchema.safeParse(candidate);
      if (!validated.success) {
        status = "retried";
        jsonRetries += 1;
        if (jsonRetries > 1) {
          throw new Error(`Agent JSON failed validation twice: ${validated.error.message}`);
        }
        scratchpad.push(`Previous JSON failed schema validation: ${validated.error.message}. Retry.`);
        steps.push({
          index: stepIndex,
          kind: "plan",
          payload: candidate,
          startedAt: stepStart,
          endedAt: Date.now(),
          phase,
          tokensIn,
          tokensOut,
          status,
        });
        opts.callbacks.onStep(steps[steps.length - 1]);
        continue;
      }

      parsed = validated.data;
      jsonRetries = 0;
    } finally {
      try { await sequence.dispose(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
    }

    if (!parsed) continue;

    // ---- Execute the tool call ----
    if (parsed.kind === "finish") {
      const step: AgentStep = {
        index: stepIndex,
        kind: "finish",
        payload: { summary: parsed.summary },
        startedAt: stepStart,
        endedAt: Date.now(),
        phase,
        tokensIn,
        tokensOut,
        status: "ok",
      };
      steps.push(step);
      opts.callbacks.onStep(step);
      opts.callbacks.onFinish(parsed.summary, steps);
      return;
    }

    if (parsed.kind === "read") {
      const rel = parsed.path.replace(/^\.\//, "");
      if (seenReads.has(rel)) {
        scratchpad.push(`Already read ${rel}; do not read it again.`);
        const step: AgentStep = {
          index: stepIndex, kind: "read", payload: { path: rel, repeat: true },
          startedAt: stepStart, endedAt: Date.now(), phase, tokensIn, tokensOut, status: "ok",
        };
        steps.push(step);
        opts.callbacks.onStep(step);
        continue;
      }
      try {
        const content = await opts.io.readFile(path.join(opts.workspaceRoot, rel));
        seenReads.add(rel);
        accumulatedFiles.set(rel, content);
        scratchpad.push(`read ${rel}:\n${content.slice(0, 4000)}`);
        const step: AgentStep = {
          index: stepIndex, kind: "read", payload: { path: rel },
          startedAt: stepStart, endedAt: Date.now(), phase, tokensIn, tokensOut, status: "ok",
        };
        steps.push(step);
        opts.callbacks.onStep(step);
      } catch (err) {
        const step: AgentStep = {
          index: stepIndex, kind: "read", payload: { path: rel, error: String(err) },
          startedAt: stepStart, endedAt: Date.now(), phase, tokensIn, tokensOut, status: "failed",
        };
        steps.push(step);
        opts.callbacks.onStep(step);
        scratchpad.push(`Failed to read ${rel}: ${String(err)}`);
      }
      continue;
    }

    if (parsed.kind === "terminal") {
      if (stepTimeoutMs === 0) {
        // Tier 1 never reaches here because agent mode is unavailable, but
        // guard anyway.
        scratchpad.push("Terminal is unavailable on this hardware tier.");
        continue;
      }
      const decision = await opts.callbacks.onTerminalRequest({
        requestId: crypto.randomUUID(),
        command: parsed.command,
        cwd: parsed.cwd,
        reason: parsed.reason,
      });
      const step: AgentStep = {
        index: stepIndex,
        kind: "terminal",
        payload: { command: parsed.command, cwd: parsed.cwd, decision },
        startedAt: stepStart,
        endedAt: Date.now(),
        phase,
        tokensIn,
        tokensOut,
        status: decision === "allow" ? "ok" : "aborted",
      };
      steps.push(step);
      opts.callbacks.onStep(step);
      if (decision === "deny") {
        scratchpad.push(`Terminal command denied by user: ${parsed.command}`);
      } else {
        // M6 does not execute the command. It records the approval and tells
        // the model the command was approved but not run. Execution is M7+,
        // after the sandbox lands. This is deliberate: shipping command
        // execution before the OS-level sandbox is a CVE waiting to happen.
        scratchpad.push(
          `Terminal command approved by user but execution is not yet enabled: ${parsed.command}`,
        );
      }
      continue;
    }

    if (parsed.kind === "search_replace") {
      // Ensure the Agent has read every target file before staging.
      for (const b of parsed.blocks) {
        if (!accumulatedFiles.has(b.file)) {
          try {
            accumulatedFiles.set(
              b.file,
              await opts.io.readFile(path.join(opts.workspaceRoot, b.file)),
            );
          } catch (err) {
            scratchpad.push(`Cannot read ${b.file}: ${String(err)}`);
          }
        }
      }

      const stageable = parsed.blocks.filter((b) => accumulatedFiles.has(b.file));
      if (stageable.length === 0) {
        scratchpad.push("None of the target files could be read; cannot stage.");
        continue;
      }

      accumulatedBlocks = accumulatedBlocks.concat(stageable);

      // --- Tier 2.5 fallback check ---
      const distinctFiles = new Set(accumulatedBlocks.map((b) => b.file));
      const overFileBudget = distinctFiles.size > caps.maxFilesPerAgentRun;
      const overTime = Date.now() - stepStart > stepTimeoutMs;

      if ((overFileBudget || overTime) && !fallbackTriggered && opts.tier === 2.5) {
        fallbackTriggered = true;
        // Stage what we have, then emit the rest as sequential cards.
        const stageResult = await stageBlocks(accumulatedBlocks, {
          taskId: opts.taskId,
          files: accumulatedFiles,
        });
        opts.callbacks.onDiff(stageResult.staged, stageResult.perFilePreview);
        const remaining = distinctFiles.size > caps.maxFilesPerAgentRun
          ? Array.from(distinctFiles).slice(caps.maxFilesPerAgentRun).map((file) => ({
              file,
              instruction: `Apply the remaining edit to ${file} as a separate step.`,
            }))
          : [];
        opts.callbacks.onFallback(remaining);
        const step: AgentStep = {
          index: stepIndex, kind: "search_replace",
          payload: { fallback: true, staged: stageResult.staged.id },
          startedAt: stepStart, endedAt: Date.now(), phase, tokensIn, tokensOut, status: "ok",
        };
        steps.push(step);
        opts.callbacks.onStep(step);
        opts.callbacks.onFinish("Tier 2.5 fallback: staged partial diff, queued remaining steps", steps);
        return;
      }

      // --- Single bounded syntax retry ---
      const stageResult = await stageBlocks(accumulatedBlocks, {
        taskId: opts.taskId,
        files: accumulatedFiles,
      });

      if (!stageResult.staged.validation.syntaxOk) {
        if (syntaxRetries >= 1) {
          opts.callbacks.onDiff(stageResult.staged, stageResult.perFilePreview);
          const step: AgentStep = {
            index: stepIndex, kind: "search_replace",
            payload: { syntaxFailed: true, errors: stageResult.staged.validation.errors },
            startedAt: stepStart, endedAt: Date.now(), phase, tokensIn, tokensOut, status: "failed",
          };
          steps.push(step);
          opts.callbacks.onStep(step);
          opts.callbacks.onFinish(
            "Staged diff has syntax errors after one retry; review before applying",
            steps,
          );
          return;
        }
        syntaxRetries += 1;
        const diagnostics = stageResult.staged.validation.errors.join("; ");
        scratchpad.push(
          `Syntax validation failed: ${diagnostics}. Emit a corrected search_replace block.`,
        );
        // Drop the invalid blocks so the next turn re-emits them.
        accumulatedBlocks = accumulatedBlocks.slice(0, accumulatedBlocks.length - stageable.length);
        const step: AgentStep = {
          index: stepIndex, kind: "search_replace",
          payload: { syntaxRetry: true, errors: stageResult.staged.validation.errors },
          startedAt: stepStart, endedAt: Date.now(), phase, tokensIn, tokensOut, status: "retried",
        };
        steps.push(step);
        opts.callbacks.onStep(step);
        continue;
      }

      opts.callbacks.onDiff(stageResult.staged, stageResult.perFilePreview);
      const step: AgentStep = {
        index: stepIndex, kind: "search_replace",
        payload: { staged: stageResult.staged.id, blocks: stageable.length },
        startedAt: stepStart, endedAt: Date.now(), phase, tokensIn, tokensOut, status: "ok",
      };
      steps.push(step);
      opts.callbacks.onStep(step);
      scratchpad.push(
        `Staged ${stageable.length} block(s) across ${new Set(stageable.map((b) => b.file)).size} file(s). Continue or finish.`,
      );
      continue;
    }
  }

  // Step budget exhausted without finish.
  opts.callbacks.onFinish(
    `Reached step budget (${maxSteps}) without finishing. Review staged diff.`,
    steps,
  );
}
3. src/main/ipc/agent.ipc.ts
ts
import { ipcMain } from "electron";
import path from "node:path";
import fs from "node:fs/promises";
import { CH } from "./channels.js";
import { runAgent } from "../../core/agent.js";
import { db } from "../db/index.js";
import { classifyPath } from "../../core/security.js";
import type {
  AgentStep,
  HardwareTier,
  StagedDiff,
} from "../../shared/types.js";

const activeAborts = new Map<string, AbortController>();

export function registerAgentIpc(): void {
  ipcMain.handle(
    CH.AGENT_START,
    async (
      event,
      payload: {
        taskId: string;
        userMessage: string;
        tier: HardwareTier;
        workspaceRoot: string;
        modelId: string;
        openFilePath: string | null;
        openFileLine: number | null;
      },
    ) => {
      const sender = event.sender;
      const ctrl = new AbortController();
      activeAborts.set(payload.taskId, ctrl);

      const insertStep = db.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO agent_steps
           (task_id, step_index, kind, payload_json, phase, started_at, ended_at,
            tokens_in, tokens_out, status)
         VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
      );

      const io = {
        readFile: async (abs: string): Promise<string> => {
          const d = classifyPath(abs, "read", { workspaceRoot: payload.workspaceRoot });
          if (d.action === "deny") throw new Error(`Read denied: ${d.reason}`);
          return fs.readFile(abs, "utf-8");
        },
        listDir: async (abs: string) => {
          const entries = await fs.readdir(abs, { withFileTypes: true });
          return entries.map((e) => ({ name: e.name, isDir: e.isDirectory() }));
        },
        writeFile: async (abs: string, content: string): Promise<void> => {
          const d = classifyPath(abs, "write", { workspaceRoot: payload.workspaceRoot });
          if (d.action !== "allow") {
            throw new Error(`Write blocked by security: ${d.reason}`);
          }
          await fs.writeFile(abs, content, "utf-8");
        },
      };

      void runAgent({
        taskId: payload.taskId,
        userMessage: payload.userMessage,
        tier: payload.tier,
        workspaceRoot: payload.workspaceRoot,
        modelId: payload.modelId,
        openFilePath: payload.openFilePath,
        openFileLine: payload.openFileLine,
        io,
        abortSignal: ctrl.signal,
        callbacks: {
          onStep: (step: AgentStep) => {
            insertStep.run(
              payload.taskId, step.index, step.kind, JSON.stringify(step.payload),
              step.phase, step.startedAt, step.endedAt, step.tokensIn, step.tokensOut, step.status,
            );
            if (!sender.isDestroyed()) sender.send(CH.AGENT_STEP, step);
          },
          onToken: (streamKey, text) => {
            if (!sender.isDestroyed()) sender.send(CH.AGENT_TOKEN, { streamKey, text });
          },
          onDiff: (staged: StagedDiff, previews) => {
            if (!sender.isDestroyed()) {
              sender.send(CH.AGENT_DIFF, {
                staged,
                previews: Object.fromEntries(previews),
              });
            }
          },
          onTerminalRequest: (req) =>
            new Promise<"allow" | "deny">((resolve) => {
              const handler = (
                _e: unknown,
                resp: { requestId: string; decision: "allow" | "deny" },
              ): void => {
                if (resp.requestId !== req.requestId) return;
                ipcMain.removeListener(CH.TERMINAL_RESPONSE, handler);
                resolve(resp.decision);
              };
              ipcMain.on(CH.TERMINAL_RESPONSE, handler);
              if (!sender.isDestroyed()) sender.send(CH.TERMINAL_REQUEST, req);
            }),
          onFallback: (cards) => {
            if (!sender.isDestroyed()) sender.send(CH.AGENT_FALLBACK, { cards });
          },
          onFinish: (summary, steps) => {
            activeAborts.delete(payload.taskId);
            if (!sender.isDestroyed()) sender.send(CH.AGENT_FINISH, { summary, steps });
          },
          onError: (err) => {
            activeAborts.delete(payload.taskId);
            if (!sender.isDestroyed()) sender.send(CH.AGENT_ERROR, { message: err.message });
          },
        },
      }).catch((err) => {
        activeAborts.delete(payload.taskId);
        if (!sender.isDestroyed()) {
          sender.send(CH.AGENT_ERROR, {
            message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),
          });
        }
      });

      return { taskId: payload.taskId };
    },
  );

  ipcMain.handle(CH.AGENT_ABORT, async (_e, taskId: string) => {
    const ctrl = activeAborts.get(taskId);
    if (!ctrl) return { aborted: false };
    ctrl.abort();
    return { aborted: true };
  });
}

4. Terminal approval — default-deny, session-scoped
The security model follows the VS Code / Copilot pattern: terminal commands
require explicit approval before they run, with a dialog showing the exact
command, and there is no persistent auto-approve toggle in v1[reference:2].
requiresTerminalApproval in M4 already returns true unconditionally. M6
implements the dialog and the response channel, but does not execute the
command. This is deliberate:

The OS-level sandbox (network namespaces, filesystem jail) does not exist
yet. Executing shell commands before that sandbox ships is the CVE-2025-
53773 class of mistake.

The Agent records the approval decision in AgentStep and in the audit_log
table. M7 wires execution behind the sandbox.

src/renderer/dialogs/TerminalApproval.tsx
tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

interface Request {
  requestId: string;
  command: string;
  cwd: string;
  reason: string;
}

export function TerminalApproval(): JSX.Element | null {
  const [req, setReq] = useState<Request | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const off = window.thunder.terminal.onRequest((r) => setReq(r));
    return off;
  }, []);

  if (!req) return null;

  const respond = async (decision: "allow" | "deny"): Promise<void> => {
    await window.thunder.terminal.respond({ requestId: req.requestId, decision });
    setReq(null);
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/40">
      <div className="w-[560px] rounded bg-white p-4 shadow-lg">
        <h3 className="mb-2 text-lg font-semibold">Terminal command approval</h3>
        <div className="mb-2 rounded bg-gray-100 p-2 font-mono text-sm">
          <div className="text-xs text-gray-500">cwd: {req.cwd}</div>
          <div className="mt-1 whitespace-pre-wrap break-all">{req.command}</div>
        </div>
        <p className="mb-3 text-sm text-gray-700">{req.reason}</p>
        <p className="mb-3 text-xs text-gray-500">
          Execution is not enabled in this build. Approving records the decision;
          the command will not run.
        </p>
        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
          <button
            className="rounded bg-gray-200 px-3 py-1 text-sm"
            onClick={() => void respond("deny")}
          >
            Reject
          </button>
          <button
            className="rounded bg-blue-600 px-3 py-1 text-sm text-white"
            onClick={() => void respond("allow")}
          >
            Approve (record only)
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
5. Preload additions for Agent and terminal
ts
import type {
  AgentStep,
  StagedDiff,
  HardwareTier,
} from "../shared/types.js";

// inside api:
agent: {
  start: (payload: {
    taskId: string;
    userMessage: string;
    tier: HardwareTier;
    workspaceRoot: string;
    modelId: string;
    openFilePath: string | null;
    openFileLine: number | null;
  }): Promise<{ taskId: string }> =>
    ipcRenderer.invoke(CH.AGENT_START, payload),
  abort: (taskId: string): Promise<{ aborted: boolean }> =>
    ipcRenderer.invoke(CH.AGENT_ABORT, taskId),
  onStep: (cb: (s: AgentStep) => void): (() => void) => {
    const h = (_e: unknown, s: AgentStep): void => cb(s);
    ipcRenderer.on(CH.AGENT_STEP, h);
    return () => ipcRenderer.removeListener(CH.AGENT_STEP, h);
  },
  onToken: (cb: (p: { streamKey: string; text: string }) => void): (() => void) => {
    const h = (_e: unknown, p: { streamKey: string; text: string }): void => cb(p);
    ipcRenderer.on(CH.AGENT_TOKEN, h);
    return () => ipcRenderer.removeListener(CH.AGENT_TOKEN, h);
  },
  onDiff: (cb: (p: {
    staged: StagedDiff;
    previews: Record<string, { before: string; after: string }>;
  }) => void): (() => void) => {
    const h = (_e: unknown, p: { staged: StagedDiff; previews: Record<string, { before: string; after: string }> }): void => cb(p);
    ipcRenderer.on(CH.AGENT_DIFF, h);
    return () => ipcRenderer.removeListener(CH.AGENT_DIFF, h);
  },
  onFallback: (cb: (p: { cards: Array<{ file: string; instruction: string }> }) => void): (() => void) => {
    const h = (_e: unknown, p: { cards: Array<{ file: string; instruction: string }> }): void => cb(p);
    ipcRenderer.on(CH.AGENT_FALLBACK, h);
    return () => ipcRenderer.removeListener(CH.AGENT_FALLBACK, h);
  },
  onFinish: (cb: (p: { summary: string; steps: AgentStep[] }) => void): (() => void) => {
    const h = (_e: unknown, p: { summary: string; steps: AgentStep[] }): void => cb(p);
    ipcRenderer.on(CH.AGENT_FINISH, h);
    return () => ipcRenderer.removeListener(CH.AGENT_FINISH, h);
  },
  onError: (cb: (p: { message: string }) => void): (() => void) => {
    const h = (_e: unknown, p: { message: string }): void => cb(p);
    ipcRenderer.on(CH.AGENT_ERROR, h);
    return () => ipcRenderer.removeListener(CH.AGENT_ERROR, h);
  },
},
terminal: {
  onRequest: (cb: (r: {
    requestId: string;
    command: string;
    cwd: string;
    reason: string;
  }) => void): (() => void) => {
    const h = (_e: unknown, r: { requestId: string; command: string; cwd: string; reason: string }): void => cb(r);
    ipcRenderer.on(CH.TERMINAL_REQUEST, h);
    return () => ipcRenderer.removeListener(CH.TERMINAL_REQUEST, h);
  },
  respond: (p: { requestId: string; decision: "allow" | "deny" }): Promise<{ ok: boolean }> =>
    ipcRenderer.invoke(CH.TERMINAL_RESPONSE, p),
},
6. Agent mode in ChatPanel.tsx
Add an agent branch. The key behaviors:

Generate a taskId with crypto.randomUUID().

Call window.thunder.agent.start(...).

Subscribe to step / diff / fallback / finish events and update local state.

Render a compact step ledger (one line per step, matching the
basecradle-harness pattern of a live step counter).

On onDiff, show DiffReview in the right pane.

On onFallback, render "Next Step" cards below the diff.

This is UI wiring, not core logic. The M6 acceptance criteria below verify the
core loop, not the polish of this panel.

7. tests/unit/agent.test.ts
ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from "vitest";

vi.mock("electron", () => ({
  app: { isPackaged: false, getAppPath: () => process.cwd(), getPath: () => "/tmp" },
}));

const prompts: string[] = [];
let nextResponse: string | (() => string) = '{"kind":"finish","summary":"done"}';

vi.mock("node-llama-cpp", () => {
  class MockSequence {
    tokenMeter = { getState: () => ({ usedInputTokens: 10, usedOutputTokens: 5 }) };
    async dispose(): Promise<void> {}
  }
  class MockContext {
    getSequence(): MockSequence { return new MockSequence(); }
  }
  class MockModel {
    async createContext(): Promise<MockContext> { return new MockContext(); }
  }
  return {
    getLlama: async () => ({
      loadModel: async () => new MockModel(),
      createGrammarForJsonSchema: async () => ({}),
    }),
    LlamaChatSession: class {
      constructor(_o: unknown) {}
      async prompt(p: string, _opts: unknown): Promise<string> {
        prompts.push(p);
        return typeof nextResponse === "function" ? nextResponse() : nextResponse;
      }
    },
  };
});

vi.mock("../../src/core/tree-sitter.js", () => ({
  validateSyntax: async () => ({ ok: true, errors: [] }),
  languageForFile: () => null,
}));

import { runAgent } from "../../src/core/agent.js";
import type { AgentStep } from "../../src/shared/types.js";

function io(files: Record<string, string>) {
  return {
    readFile: async (abs: string): Promise<string> => {
      const key = Object.keys(files).find((k) => abs.endsWith(k));
      if (!key) throw new Error(`no file ${abs}`);
      return files[key];
    },
    listDir: async () => [],
    writeFile: async () => {},
  };
}

function collector() {
  const steps: AgentStep[] = [];
  const diffs: unknown[] = [];
  let finished: { summary: string; steps: AgentStep[] } | null = null;
  let errored: Error | null = null;
  const callbacks = {
    onStep: (s: AgentStep) => { steps.push(s); },
    onToken: () => {},
    onDiff: (staged: unknown, previews: unknown) => { diffs.push({ staged, previews }); },
    onTerminalRequest: async () => "deny" as const,
    onFallback: () => {},
    onFinish: (summary: string, s: AgentStep[]) => { finished = { summary, steps: s }; },
    onError: (e: Error) => { errored = e; },
  };
  return { steps, diffs, callbacks, getFinished: () => finished, getError: () => errored };
}

describe("runAgent", () => {
  it("finishes immediately when the model emits finish", async () => {
    nextResponse = '{"kind":"finish","summary":"nothing to do"}';
    prompts.length = 0;
    const c = collector();
    await runAgent({
      taskId: "t1", userMessage: "hi", tier: 2,
      workspaceRoot: "/tmp/ws", modelId: "m",
      openFilePath: null, openFileLine: null,
      io: io({}), abortSignal: new AbortController().signal,
      callbacks: c.callbacks,
    });
    expect(c.getFinished()?.summary).toBe("nothing to do");
    expect(c.steps).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(c.steps[0].kind).toBe("finish");
  });

  it("retries once on non-JSON output, then aborts", async () => {
    nextResponse = "this is not json";
    const c = collector();
    await runAgent({
      taskId: "t2", userMessage: "hi", tier: 2,
      workspaceRoot: "/tmp/ws", modelId: "m",
      openFilePath: null, openFileLine: null,
      io: io({}), abortSignal: new AbortController().signal,
      callbacks: c.callbacks,
    });
    expect(c.getError()?.message).toMatch(/JSON twice|not valid JSON/i);
  });

  it("stages a search_replace block and reports a diff", async () => {
    nextResponse = JSON.stringify({
      kind: "search_replace",
      blocks: [{ file: "a.ts", search: "const x = 1;", replace: "const x = 2;" }],
    });
    const c = collector();
    let responded = false;
    // After the first search_replace, finish.
    const origPrompt = prompts.length;
    void origPrompt;
    const responses = [
      nextResponse,
      '{"kind":"finish","summary":"done"}',
    ];
    let i = 0;
    nextResponse = () => responses[Math.min(i++, responses.length - 1)];
    await runAgent({
      taskId: "t3", userMessage: "change x", tier: 2,
      workspaceRoot: "/tmp/ws", modelId: "m",
      openFilePath: null, openFileLine: null,
      io: io({ "a.ts": "const x = 1;\n" }),
      abortSignal: new AbortController().signal,
      callbacks: c.callbacks,
    });
    expect(c.diffs.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
    const kinds = c.steps.map((s) => s.kind);
    expect(kinds).toContain("search_replace");
    expect(kinds).toContain("finish");
    responded = true;
    expect(responded).toBe(true);
  });

  it("falls back on Tier 2.5 when file budget is exceeded", async () => {
    const blocks = [1, 2, 3].map((n) => ({
      file: `f${n}.ts`,
      search: "const a = 1;",
      replace: "const a = 2;",
    }));
    const responses = [
      JSON.stringify({ kind: "search_replace", blocks }),
      '{"kind":"finish","summary":"done"}',
    ];
    let i = 0;
    nextResponse = () => responses[Math.min(i++, responses.length - 1)];
    const fallbackEvents: unknown[] = [];
    const c = collector();
    c.callbacks.onFallback = (cards: unknown) => { fallbackEvents.push(cards); };
    await runAgent({
      taskId: "t4", userMessage: "edit 3 files", tier: 2.5,
      workspaceRoot: "/tmp/ws", modelId: "m",
      openFilePath: null, openFileLine: null,
      io: io({
        "f1.ts": "const a = 1;\n",
        "f2.ts": "const a = 1;\n",
        "f3.ts": "const a = 1;\n",
      }),
      abortSignal: new AbortController().signal,
      callbacks: c.callbacks,
    });
    expect(fallbackEvents.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
    expect(c.diffs.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
  });

  it("denies terminal execution but records the decision", async () => {
    const responses = [
      JSON.stringify({ kind: "terminal", command: "ls", cwd: ".", reason: "list files" }),
      '{"kind":"finish","summary":"done"}',
    ];
    let i = 0;
    nextResponse = () => responses[Math.min(i++, responses.length - 1)];
    const c = collector();
    await runAgent({
      taskId: "t5", userMessage: "ls", tier: 2,
      workspaceRoot: "/tmp/ws", modelId: "m",
      openFilePath: null, openFileLine: null,
      io: io({}), abortSignal: new AbortController().signal,
      callbacks: c.callbacks,
    });
    const terminalStep = c.steps.find((s) => s.kind === "terminal");
    expect(terminalStep).toBeDefined();
    expect((terminalStep?.payload as { decision: string }).decision).toBe("deny");
  });
});
8. M6 acceptance checklist
bash
pnpm build:main
pnpm build:renderer
pnpm test tests/unit/agent.test.ts
# EXPECT: 5 tests pass
pnpm lint
pnpm exec tsc -p tsconfig.node.json --noEmit
Manual smoke test (Tier 2 or Tier 3 machine, since Agent is unavailable on Tier 1):

text
1. Launch with a real workspace. Confirm the model is downloaded.
2. Switch chat mode to Agent. Ask:
     "Add input validation to the parseConfig function in src/config.ts"
3. Watch the step ledger. EXPECT:
   - read steps for src/config.ts (and possibly its imports)
   - a search_replace step
   - a DiffReview panel with syntaxOk === true
   - a finish step
4. Check the SQLite table:
     sqlite3 <userData>/thunder.db "SELECT step_index, kind, phase, status FROM agent_steps ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 20;"
   EXPECT: planning phase on the first read step, edit-gen on the
   search_replace step. If every step says planning, the phase tagging is
   wrong; fix before M7.
5. Ask the Agent to run a terminal command:
     "Run pnpm test and tell me if it passes"
   EXPECT: a terminal approval dialog with the exact command, cwd, and
   reason. Click Reject. The AgentStep for the terminal step has
   status = "aborted". Clicking Approve records status = "ok" but the
   command is NOT executed. Verify with `ps` or by checking the command's
   side effects (there should be none).
6. Force a JSON failure: temporarily set the system prompt to something that
   makes the model emit prose. EXPECT: exactly one retry, then a clean
   AGENT_ERROR. Do not allow an infinite loop.
7. Force a syntax failure: stage a block whose replacement breaks syntax
   (e.g. remove a closing brace). EXPECT: one bounded retry with the
   tree-sitter diagnostic; on the second failure, the staged diff is
   surfaced with syntaxOk === false and "Accept all" disabled.
8. Tier 2.5 fallback: on a 16GB CPU-only machine, ask the Agent to edit 3
   files. EXPECT: after the first validated file, the loop stops and
   emits "Next Step" cards for the remaining files. The laptop must not
   freeze.
9. Redaction check: put a fake `sk-ant-...` key in an open file, run an
   Agent task that reads it. Inspect the prompt via the main-process log.
   EXPECT: the key is replaced with [REDACTED] before the prompt is built.
10. Abort mid-loop: start a task, click Cancel during generation.
    EXPECT: the loop stops within one step, onFinish fires with
    "Aborted by user", no orphaned sequences, and the next Agent task
    works normally.
M6 is complete when all 10 manual steps pass and all 5 automated tests are green.

What M6 deliberately does not do (and why)
Terminal commands are not executed. The approval is recorded, but the
command does not run. Execution requires the OS-level sandbox (network
namespace, filesystem jail) that lands in M7. Shipping execution before the
sandbox is the CVE-2025-53773 class of mistake. The dialog tells the user
this explicitly.

No automatic multi-file Agent beyond maxFilesPerAgentRun. Tier 2 and
2.5 cap at 2 files; Tier 3 caps at 8. The fallback is the feature, not a
workaround. A 7B model that "tries harder" on a 16GB CPU-only machine
produces a frozen laptop, not a better edit.

No token-meter integration for context budget. buildContext caps the
open file at 4,000 characters and the scratchpad grows unbounded. M7
replaces this with node-llama-cpp's tokenizer and a hard context budget.
This is acceptable for M6 because the step budget already bounds total
work; the context budget is a quality optimization, not a safety gate.

No Plan→Agent handoff. The Agent is invoked directly from Agent mode.
The Plan mode from M5 produces a plan but does not feed it into the Agent
as a pre-approved step sequence. That handoff is M7 polish, after the loop
is proven.

Reply "M6 done" and I will provide M7 (entitlements wiring, model Lab gating,
offline license verification, and the SQLite-backed waitlist).

text
This response is AI-generated, for reference only.


markdown
# THUNDER — Continuation Spec (M7)
# Paste this AFTER M6. This spec is M7 only:
# entitlement model, Ed25519-signed license verification, offline grace,
# SQLite license state, entitlement IPC, and Pro feature gating in the renderer.
# Do not begin M7 until the M6 acceptance checklist is green.

## M7 scope

M7 delivers:
1. `src/shared/license.ts` — license payload schema + plan constants
2. `src/core/entitlements.ts` — Ed25519 verification, offline grace, state
3. `scripts/generate-keypair.ts` — dev-only key generation (do not commit private key)
4. `scripts/sign-license.ts` — dev-only license issuance
5. `src/main/db/schema.sql` — add `license_state` table (migration)
6. `src/main/ipc/entitlements.ipc.ts` — activate / get / refresh IPC
7. `src/main/entitlement-scheduler.ts` — 30-day online check stub + zero-outbound guard
8. Renderer: `store/entitlement.ts`, `ProGate.tsx`, Model Lab preview tab
9. `tests/unit/entitlements.test.ts`

M7 does NOT wire payment. There is no checkout, no webhook receiver, no MoR
integration. Licenses are issued by the developer manually with
`scripts/sign-license.ts` and validated entirely offline. This is the correct
MVP shape: entitlements exist before revenue exists, so that when MoR lands
in M10, only the *issuance* path changes, not the *verification* path.

## Milestone honesty note (read this before starting)

The earlier plan said M7 would absorb the deferred items from M6 (token-meter
context budget, Plan→Agent handoff, terminal execution sandbox). It is not
absorbing them. Here is why, stated plainly:

- **Terminal execution sandbox** is a 400–800 line OS-specific feature
  (`seccomp`/`landlock` on Linux, `sandbox_init` on macOS, Job Objects on
  Windows). Folding it into M7 would triple this milestone's size and delay
  shipping the MVP. It moves to **M10 backlog**. v1 ships with terminal
  execution **disabled** — a valid, defensible product decision, and the
  safest default.
- **Token-meter context budget** requires re-plumbing `buildContext` to
  call `llama.tokenize(...)`. It is a quality improvement, not a blocker.
  **M8 backlog**.
- **Plan→Agent handoff** is UI polish. **M8 backlog**.

If you disagree with this deferral, stop now and re-scope M7. Otherwise the
MVP definition of done is: entitlements + waitlist + packaging (M7–M9),
with terminal execution disabled and MoR issuance out of scope.

## 1. `src/shared/license.ts`

```ts
export type LicensePlan = "annual" | "monthly" | "team";
export type LicenseTier = "pro";

export interface LicensePayload {
  email: string;
  plan: LicensePlan;
  tier: LicenseTier;
  issuedAt: number;      // unix seconds
  expiresAt: number;     // unix seconds
  // Ed25519 signature over the canonical JSON of the payload minus this field.
  signature: string;     // base64url
}

export const OFFLINE_GRACE_DAYS = 7;
export const ONLINE_CHECK_INTERVAL_DAYS = 30;

// Hard-coded Pro features. Adding a key here requires updating the M4 test
// suite (fixture 20 in security.test.ts) and the renderer ProGate map.
export const PRO_FEATURES = [
  "modelLab",
  "manualPin",
  "perfTuning",
  "byokFallback",
  "teamPolicy",
  "diagnosticBundle",
] as const;

export type ProFeature = (typeof PRO_FEATURES)[number];
2. src/core/entitlements.ts
ts
import crypto from "node:crypto";
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { app } from "electron";
import type {
  Entitlement,
  ProFeatureKey,
} from "../shared/types.js";
import {
  OFFLINE_GRACE_DAYS,
  PRO_FEATURES,
  type LicensePayload,
} from "../shared/license.js";

// ---------- Public key loading ----------

let cachedPublicKey: crypto.KeyObject | null = null;

function publicKeyPath(): string {
  return app.isPackaged
    ? path.join(process.resourcesPath, "thunder-public-key.pem")
    : path.join(app.getAppPath(), "public", "thunder-public-key.pem");
}

function getPublicKey(): crypto.KeyObject {
  if (cachedPublicKey) return cachedPublicKey;
  const pem = fs.readFileSync(publicKeyPath(), "utf-8");
  cachedPublicKey = crypto.createPublicKey(pem);
  return cachedPublicKey;
}

// ---------- Canonical payload ----------

/**
 * The signature is over the canonical JSON of the payload minus signature.
 * Key order is fixed by JSON.stringify iteration order of the object literal
 * below. Do not reorder.
 */
function canonicalPayload(p: Omit<LicensePayload, "signature">): string {
  return JSON.stringify({
    email: p.email,
    plan: p.plan,
    tier: p.tier,
    issuedAt: p.issuedAt,
    expiresAt: p.expiresAt,
  });
}

// ---------- Verification ----------

export interface VerifyResult {
  ok: boolean;
  payload?: LicensePayload;
  reason?: string;
}

export function verifyLicense(licenseJson: string): VerifyResult {
  let parsed: LicensePayload;
  try {
    parsed = JSON.parse(licenseJson) as LicensePayload;
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, reason: "malformed JSON" };
  }

  if (
    typeof parsed.email !== "string" ||
    typeof parsed.plan !== "string" ||
    typeof parsed.tier !== "string" ||
    typeof parsed.issuedAt !== "number" ||
    typeof parsed.expiresAt !== "number" ||
    typeof parsed.signature !== "string"
  ) {
    return { ok: false, reason: "payload shape invalid" };
  }

  const canonical = canonicalPayload({
    email: parsed.email,
    plan: parsed.plan,
    tier: parsed.tier,
    issuedAt: parsed.issuedAt,
    expiresAt: parsed.expiresAt,
  });

  let sigBuf: Buffer;
  try {
    sigBuf = Buffer.from(parsed.signature, "base64url");
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, reason: "signature not base64url" };
  }

  const pk = getPublicKey();
  let valid: boolean;
  try {
    valid = crypto.verify(
      null, // Ed25519 does not use a hash algorithm parameter
      Buffer.from(canonical, "utf-8"),
      pk,
      sigBuf,
    );
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, reason: "signature verification threw" };
  }

  if (!valid) return { ok: false, reason: "signature invalid" };

  const nowSec = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  if (parsed.expiresAt < nowSec) {
    return { ok: false, reason: `expired at ${parsed.expiresAt}`, payload: parsed };
  }

  return { ok: true, payload: parsed };
}

// ---------- Entitlement state ----------

export interface LicenseState {
  licenseJson: string | null;
  lastCheck: number | null;      // unix seconds
  offlineGraceUntil: number | null;
}

const FREE_ENTITLEMENT: Entitlement = {
  tier: "free",
  plan: "none",
  expiresAt: null,
  offlineGraceUntil: 0,
  features: PRO_FEATURES.reduce(
    (acc, k) => {
      acc[k] = false;
      return acc;
    },
    {} as Record<ProFeatureKey, boolean>,
  ),
};

export function entitlementFromState(state: LicenseState): Entitlement {
  if (!state.licenseJson) return FREE_ENTITLEMENT;

  const verified = verifyLicense(state.licenseJson);
  if (!verified.ok || !verified.payload) {
    // If we still have an offline grace window, allow Pro features until it
    // expires. This is what keeps offline / air-gapped users working.
    const nowSec = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
    if (
      state.offlineGraceUntil !== null &&
      nowSec < state.offlineGraceUntil
    ) {
      return proEntitlement(
        state.offlineGraceUntil,
        state.licenseJson,
      );
    }
    return FREE_ENTITLEMENT;
  }

  return proEntitlement(
    state.offlineGraceUntil ?? verified.payload.expiresAt,
    state.licenseJson,
  );
}

function proEntitlement(
  offlineGraceUntil: number,
  _licenseJson: string,
): Entitlement {
  // We do not store the payload on the Entitlement object because it may
  // contain PII (email). Callers that need the email read it from the DB.
  return {
    tier: "pro",
    plan: "annual",
    expiresAt: null,
    offlineGraceUntil,
    features: PRO_FEATURES.reduce(
      (acc, k) => {
        acc[k] = true;
        return acc;
      },
      {} as Record<ProFeatureKey, boolean>,
    ),
  };
}

// ---------- Activate ----------

export interface ActivateResult {
  ok: boolean;
  entitlement?: Entitlement;
  reason?: string;
}

export function activateLicense(
  licenseJson: string,
  persist: (state: LicenseState) => void,
): ActivateResult {
  const verified = verifyLicense(licenseJson);
  if (!verified.ok || !verified.payload) {
    return { ok: false, reason: verified.reason };
  }

  const nowSec = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const state: LicenseState = {
    licenseJson,
    lastCheck: nowSec,
    offlineGraceUntil: nowSec + OFFLINE_GRACE_DAYS * 86400,
  };
  persist(state);
  return { ok: true, entitlement: entitlementFromState(state) };
}

// ---------- Feature gate ----------

export function featureEnabled(
  entitlement: Entitlement,
  feature: ProFeatureKey,
): boolean {
  return entitlement.features[feature] === true;
}
3. scripts/generate-keypair.ts
Run once, in dev. Do not commit the private key. The public key is
committed to public/thunder-public-key.pem. The private key is stored
offline (password manager, hardware token, etc.) and used only by
sign-license.ts.

ts
import crypto from "node:crypto";
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const { publicKey, privateKey } = crypto.generateKeyPairSync("ed25519");

const publicPem = publicKey.export({ type: "spki", format: "pem" });
const privatePem = privateKey.export({ type: "pkcs8", format: "pem" });

const publicPath = path.join(process.cwd(), "public", "thunder-public-key.pem");
fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(publicPath), { recursive: true });
fs.writeFileSync(publicPath, publicPem, "utf-8");

const privatePath = path.join(process.cwd(), "scripts", "thunder-private-key.pem");
if (fs.existsSync(privatePath)) {
  console.error(`Refusing to overwrite existing private key at ${privatePath}`);
  process.exit(1);
}
fs.writeFileSync(privatePath, privatePem, { mode: 0o600 });

console.log(`Public key written to ${publicPath}`);
console.log(`Private key written to ${privatePath} (DO NOT COMMIT)`);
console.log(`Add this to .gitignore: scripts/thunder-private-key.pem`);

4. scripts/sign-license.ts
Usage: pnpm tsx scripts/sign-license.ts <email> <plan> <days-valid>

ts
import crypto from "node:crypto";
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import type { LicensePayload, LicensePlan } from "../src/shared/license.js";

const [, , email, planArg, daysArg] = process.argv;

if (!email || !planArg || !daysArg) {
  console.error("Usage: sign-license.ts <email> <annual|monthly|team> <days>");
  process.exit(1);
}

const plan = planArg as LicensePlan;
if (!["annual", "monthly", "team"].includes(plan)) {
  console.error(`Invalid plan: ${plan}`);
  process.exit(1);
}

const days = Number(daysArg);
if (!Number.isInteger(days) || days <= 0) {
  console.error(`Invalid days: ${daysArg}`);
  process.exit(1);
}

const privatePath = path.join(process.cwd(), "scripts", "thunder-private-key.pem");
if (!fs.existsSync(privatePath)) {
  console.error(`Private key not found at ${privatePath}. Run generate-keypair.ts first.`);
  process.exit(1);
}

const privateKey = crypto.createPrivateKey(fs.readFileSync(privatePath, "utf-8"));

const nowSec = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
const payloadWithoutSig = {
  email,
  plan,
  tier: "pro" as const,
  issuedAt: nowSec,
  expiresAt: nowSec + days * 86400,
};
const canonical = JSON.stringify(payloadWithoutSig);
const signature = crypto
  .sign(null, Buffer.from(canonical, "utf-8"), privateKey)
  .toString("base64url");

const payload: LicensePayload = {
  ...payloadWithoutSig,
  signature,
};

const outPath = path.join(process.cwd(), `${email.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/gi, "_")}.license.json`);
fs.writeFileSync(outPath, JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2), "utf-8");
console.log(`License written to ${outPath}`);
5. SQLite migration — append to schema.sql
sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS license_state (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1),
  license_json TEXT,
  last_check INTEGER,
  offline_grace_until INTEGER
);

INSERT OR IGNORE INTO license_state (id, license_json, last_check, offline_grace_until)
VALUES (1, NULL, NULL, NULL);
schema.sql is idempotent (IF NOT EXISTS), so existing dev databases
upgrade cleanly on next launch.

6. src/main/ipc/entitlements.ipc.ts
ts
import { ipcMain } from "electron";
import { CH } from "./channels.js";
import { db } from "../db/index.js";
import {
  activateLicense,
  entitlementFromState,
  featureEnabled,
  type LicenseState,
} from "../../core/entitlements.js";
import type { Entitlement, ProFeatureKey } from "../../shared/types.js";

function readState(): LicenseState {
  const row = db
    .prepare(
      "SELECT license_json, last_check, offline_grace_until FROM license_state WHERE id = 1",
    )
    .get() as
    | {
        license_json: string | null;
        last_check: number | null;
        offline_grace_until: number | null;
      }
    | undefined;
  return {
    licenseJson: row?.license_json ?? null,
    lastCheck: row?.last_check ?? null,
    offlineGraceUntil: row?.offline_grace_until ?? null,
  };
}

function writeState(state: LicenseState): void {
  db.prepare(
    `UPDATE license_state
       SET license_json = ?, last_check = ?, offline_grace_until = ?
     WHERE id = 1`,
  ).run(state.licenseJson, state.lastCheck, state.offlineGraceUntil);
}

export function registerEntitlementsIpc(): void {
  ipcMain.handle(CH.ENTITLEMENT_GET, (): Entitlement => {
    return entitlementFromState(readState());
  });

  ipcMain.handle(
    CH.ENTITLEMENT_ACTIVATE,
    (_e, licenseJson: string) => {
      const result = activateLicense(licenseJson, writeState);
      if (!result.ok) return result;
      return result;
    },
  );

  ipcMain.handle(
    "entitlement:featureEnabled",
    (_e, feature: ProFeatureKey) => {
      const entitlement = entitlementFromState(readState());
      return { enabled: featureEnabled(entitlement, feature) };
    },
  );

  ipcMain.handle("entitlement:clear", () => {
    writeState({ licenseJson: null, lastCheck: null, offlineGraceUntil: null });
    return { ok: true };
  });
}
Add to channels.ts:

ts
ENTITLEMENT_GET: "entitlement:get",
ENTITLEMENT_ACTIVATE: "entitlement:activate",
ENTITLEMENT_FEATURE_ENABLED: "entitlement:featureEnabled",
ENTITLEMENT_CLEAR: "entitlement:clear",
7. src/main/entitlement-scheduler.ts
The 30-day online check is a stub in M7. It runs on app start and every 24
hours. If Zero Outbound Mode is on, it does nothing. If off, it calls a
configurable URL (default: https://thunder-models.pages.dev/license-check).
If the URL returns {"valid": true}, refresh offlineGraceUntil. If it
returns {"valid": false}, clear the license. If it fails or times out,
leave the state alone (grace period handles the fallback).

MoR integration replaces the URL and response schema in M10. The scheduler
itself does not change.

ts
import { app } from "electron";
import { db } from "./db/index.js";
import { isZeroOutbound } from "../core/security.js";
import { OFFLINE_GRACE_DAYS } from "../shared/license.js";

const CHECK_URL = "https://thunder-models.pages.dev/license-check";
const CHECK_INTERVAL_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
const MIN_SECONDS_BETWEEN_CHECKS = 30 * 86400;

async function runOnce(): Promise<void> {
  if (isZeroOutbound()) return;

  const row = db
    .prepare("SELECT license_json, last_check FROM license_state WHERE id = 1")
    .get() as { license_json: string | null; last_check: number | null } | undefined;
  if (!row?.license_json) return;

  const nowSec = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  if (row.last_check !== null && nowSec - row.last_check < MIN_SECONDS_BETWEEN_CHECKS) {
    return;
  }

  try {
    const res = await fetch(CHECK_URL, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ license: row.license_json }),
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000),
    });
    if (!res.ok) return;
    const body = (await res.json()) as { valid?: boolean };
    if (body.valid === true) {
      db.prepare(
        "UPDATE license_state SET last_check = ?, offline_grace_until = ? WHERE id = 1",
      ).run(nowSec, nowSec + OFFLINE_GRACE_DAYS * 86400);
    } else if (body.valid === false) {
      db.prepare(
        "UPDATE license_state SET license_json = NULL, last_check = NULL, offline_grace_until = NULL WHERE id = 1",
      ).run();
    }
  } catch {
    // Network failure: do nothing, grace period covers it.
  }
}

let timer: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;

export function startEntitlementScheduler(): void {
  if (timer) return;
  void runOnce();
  timer = setInterval(() => void runOnce(), CHECK_INTERVAL_MS);
}

export function stopEntitlementScheduler(): void {
  if (timer) {
    clearInterval(timer);
    timer = null;
  }
}
In src/main/index.ts, call startEntitlementScheduler() after
registerEntitlementsIpc(), and stopEntitlementScheduler() on before-quit.

9. Renderer gating
src/renderer/store/entitlement.ts
ts
import { create } from "zustand";
import type { Entitlement, ProFeatureKey } from "../../shared/types.js";

interface EntitlementStore {
  entitlement: Entitlement | null;
  loaded: boolean;
  load: () => Promise<void>;
  activate: (licenseJson: string) => Promise<{ ok: boolean; reason?: string }>;
  hasFeature: (feature: ProFeatureKey) => boolean;
}

export const useEntitlement = create<EntitlementStore>((set, get) => ({
  entitlement: null,
  loaded: false,
  async load() {
    const e = await window.thunder.entitlement.get();
    set({ entitlement: e, loaded: true });
  },
  async activate(licenseJson) {
    const r = await window.thunder.entitlement.activate(licenseJson);
    if (r.ok && r.entitlement) set({ entitlement: r.entitlement });
    return { ok: r.ok, reason: r.reason };
  },
  hasFeature(feature) {
    const e = get().entitlement;
    return e !== null && e.features[feature] === true;
  },
}));
src/renderer/components/ProGate.tsx
tsx
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { useEntitlement } from "../store/entitlement";
import type { ProFeatureKey } from "../../shared/types";

interface Props {
  feature: ProFeatureKey;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  children: ReactNode;
}

export function ProGate({ feature, title, description, children }: Props): JSX.Element {
  const loaded = useEntitlement((s) => s.loaded);
  const enabled = useEntitlement((s) => s.hasFeature(feature));

  if (!loaded) return <div className="p-4 text-sm">Loading…</div>;
  if (enabled) return <>{children}</>;

  return (
    <div className="rounded border border-gray-200 p-6 text-center">
      <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">{title}</h3>
      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-600">{description}</p>
      <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-gray-500">
        Available in Thunder Pro — $49/year or $6/month.
      </p>
      <a
        href="#waitlist"
        className="mt-3 inline-block rounded bg-blue-600 px-3 py-1 text-sm text-white"
      >
        Join the waitlist
      </a>
    </div>
  );
}

10. Activate license UI
Add a settings panel (can be a modal from a gear icon in the workspace
header) with a textarea for the license JSON and an “Activate” button:

tsx
// src/renderer/dialogs/ActivateLicense.tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { useEntitlement } from "../store/entitlement";

export function ActivateLicense({ onClose }: { onClose: () => void }): JSX.Element {
  const activate = useEntitlement((s) => s.activate);
  const [json, setJson] = useState("");
  const [err, setErr] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [ok, setOk] = useState(false);

  async function submit(): Promise<void> {
    setErr(null);
    const r = await activate(json);
    if (r.ok) setOk(true);
    else setErr(r.reason ?? "Activation failed");
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/40">
      <div className="w-[560px] rounded bg-white p-4 shadow-lg">
        <h3 className="mb-2 text-lg font-semibold">Activate Thunder Pro</h3>
        <textarea
          value={json}
          onChange={(e) => setJson(e.target.value)}
          rows={8}
          className="w-full rounded border p-2 font-mono text-xs"
          placeholder="Paste your .license.json contents here"
        />
        {err ? <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-600">{err}</p> : null}
        {ok ? <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-green-600">Activated.</p> : null}
        <div className="mt-3 flex justify-end gap-2">
          <button className="rounded bg-gray-200 px-3 py-1 text-sm" onClick={onClose}>
            Close
          </button>
          <button
            className="rounded bg-blue-600 px-3 py-1 text-sm text-white"
            onClick={() => void submit()}
          >
            Activate
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
11. tests/unit/entitlements.test.ts
ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, vi } from "vitest";
import crypto from "node:crypto";
import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";

// Set up an ephemeral keypair and public key BEFORE importing entitlements.
const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "thunder-ent-"));
const { publicKey, privateKey } = crypto.generateKeyPairSync("ed25519");
const publicPem = publicKey.export({ type: "spki", format: "pem" }) as string;
fs.mkdirSync(path.join(tmp, "public"), { recursive: true });
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(tmp, "public", "thunder-public-key.pem"), publicPem);

vi.mock("electron", () => ({
  app: { isPackaged: false, getAppPath: () => tmp, getPath: () => tmp },
}));

const { verifyLicense, activateLicense, entitlementFromState, featureEnabled } =
  await import("../../src/core/entitlements.js");
import { PRO_FEATURES, type LicensePayload } from "../../src/shared/license.js";

function sign(payload: Omit<LicensePayload, "signature">): string {
  const canonical = JSON.stringify({
    email: payload.email,
    plan: payload.plan,
    tier: payload.tier,
    issuedAt: payload.issuedAt,
    expiresAt: payload.expiresAt,
  });
  const signature = crypto
    .sign(null, Buffer.from(canonical, "utf-8"), privateKey)
    .toString("base64url");
  return JSON.stringify({ ...payload, signature });
}

const basePayload = {
  email: "user@example.com",
  plan: "annual" as const,
  tier: "pro" as const,
  issuedAt: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
  expiresAt: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 86400,
};

describe("verifyLicense", () => {
  it("accepts a correctly signed payload", () => {
    const r = verifyLicense(sign(basePayload));
    expect(r.ok).toBe(true);
    expect(r.payload?.email).toBe("user@example.com");
  });

  it("rejects a tampered payload", () => {
    const valid = JSON.parse(sign(basePayload)) as LicensePayload;
    valid.expiresAt += 999999;
    const r = verifyLicense(JSON.stringify(valid));
    expect(r.ok).toBe(false);
    expect(r.reason).toMatch(/signature/);
  });

  it("rejects a malformed signature", () => {
    const p = { ...basePayload, signature: "not-a-real-sig" };
    const r = verifyLicense(JSON.stringify(p));
    expect(r.ok).toBe(false);
  });

  it("rejects an expired payload", () => {
    const past = { ...basePayload, expiresAt: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) - 60 };
    const r = verifyLicense(sign(past));
    expect(r.ok).toBe(false);
    expect(r.reason).toMatch(/expired/);
  });

  it("rejects malformed JSON", () => {
    const r = verifyLicense("{");
    expect(r.ok).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("activateLicense + entitlementFromState", () => {
  it("activated license grants all Pro features", () => {
    let persisted: unknown = null;
    const r = activateLicense(sign(basePayload), (s) => { persisted = s; });
    expect(r.ok).toBe(true);
    expect(persisted).not.toBeNull();
    const e = entitlementFromState(persisted as never);
    for (const k of PRO_FEATURES) expect(e.features[k]).toBe(true);
    expect(e.tier).toBe("pro");
  });

  it("no license returns free entitlement with all features off", () => {
    const e = entitlementFromState({
      licenseJson: null, lastCheck: null, offlineGraceUntil: null,
    });
    expect(e.tier).toBe("free");
    for (const k of PRO_FEATURES) expect(e.features[k]).toBe(false);
  });

  it("offline grace allows Pro features when signature invalid but window open", () => {
    const nowSec = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
    const e = entitlementFromState({
      licenseJson: "tampered",
      lastCheck: nowSec - 86400,
      offlineGraceUntil: nowSec + 86400 * 3,
    });
    expect(e.tier).toBe("pro");
    expect(featureEnabled(e, "modelLab")).toBe(true);
  });

  it("expired grace window reverts to free", () => {
    const nowSec = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
    const e = entitlementFromState({
      licenseJson: "tampered",
      lastCheck: nowSec - 86400 * 30,
      offlineGraceUntil: nowSec - 1,
    });
    expect(e.tier).toBe("free");
  });
});

# Paste this AFTER M7. This spec is M8 only:
# waitlist UI + SQLite persistence, Model Lab browser (Pro-gated), token-meter
# context budget for the Agent loop, and the Plan→Agent handoff.
# Do not begin M8 until the M7 acceptance checklist is green.

## M8 scope

M8 delivers:
1. Waitlist UI on Pro-gated surfaces + `waitlist.ipc.ts` (SQLite-backed)
2. Model Lab browser: catalog list, filter by hardware tier, 1-click download,
   compatibility score, license display
3. Token-meter context budget: replace `ceil(chars / 3.5)` in `buildContext`
   with `llama.tokenize(...)` and enforce a hard budget per model
4. Plan→Agent handoff: "Generate diff" button that converts a Plan response
   into an Agent task with the plan text as a prior
5. `tests/unit/model-lab.test.ts` and `tests/unit/token-budget.test.ts`

M8 does NOT add payment. The waitlist remains SQLite-only. Checkout is M10.

## Milestone honesty note

Two things in this milestone are frequently underestimated:

- **Token-meter accuracy.** `node-llama-cpp`'s `tokenize` returns the same
  token count the model will actually see **only if** the model's BPE merges
  and the special-token configuration match what was used during the model's
  training. For Qwen 2.5 Coder, this is true out of the box. If you later add
  models with custom chat templates, this assumption breaks and the budget
  will be off by 5–15%. Add a fixture that asserts `tokenize(prompt).length`
  matches `usedInputTokens` on a known completion before trusting the budget.

- **Plan→Agent handoff quality.** A 7B model that writes a good *plan* does
  not automatically write good *blocks* from that plan. The handoff must
  include the plan as a labeled prior, not replace the user message. Empirically
  the best pattern is: system prompt unchanged, user message becomes
  `"Execute this plan step by step:\n<plan>\n\nOriginal request: <request>"`.
  If you skip the original request, the model loses the goal and produces
  over-planned edits. This is not obvious and will cost a day of debugging if
  you skip it.

## 1. `src/main/db/schema.sql` — waitlist index

The `waitlist` table from the main spec already exists. Add one index:

```sql
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_waitlist_plan
  ON waitlist(plan_interest, submitted_at);
No schema change to existing tables. Idempotent, safe on existing databases.

2. src/main/ipc/waitlist.ipc.ts
ts
import { ipcMain } from "electron";
import { CH } from "./channels.js";
import { db } from "../db/index.js";
import { z } from "zod";

const SubmitSchema = z.object({
  email: z.string().email(),
  planInterest: z.enum(["pro", "team"]),
});

export function registerWaitlistIpc(): void {
  ipcMain.handle(
    CH.WAITLIST_SUBMIT,
    (_e, payload: unknown) => {
      const parsed = SubmitSchema.safeParse(payload);
      if (!parsed.success) {
        return { ok: false, reason: parsed.error.message };
      }
      try {
        db.prepare(
          `INSERT OR IGNORE INTO waitlist (email, plan_interest, submitted_at)
           VALUES (?, ?, ?)`,
        ).run(parsed.data.email, parsed.data.planInterest, Date.now());
        return { ok: true };
      } catch (err) {
        return {
          ok: false,
          reason: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),
        };
      }
    },
  );

  // Local-only count. Used by the UI to show "N people waiting".
  // Never exposed outside the app.
  ipcMain.handle("waitlist:count", () => {
    const row = db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) as n FROM waitlist").get() as {
      n: number;
    };
    return { count: row.n };
  });
}
Add channels:

4. src/renderer/dialogs/WaitlistForm.tsx
tsx
import { useState } from "react";

interface Props {
  planInterest: "pro" | "team";
  onClose?: () => void;
}

export function WaitlistForm({ planInterest, onClose }: Props): JSX.Element {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<"idle" | "sending" | "done" | "error">(
    "idle",
  );
  const [reason, setReason] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function submit(): Promise<void> {
    setStatus("sending");
    setReason(null);
    const r = await window.thunder.waitlist.submit({ email, planInterest });
    if (r.ok) {
      setStatus("done");
    } else {
      setStatus("error");
      setReason(r.reason ?? "Something went wrong");
    }
  }

  if (status === "done") {
    return (
      <div className="p-3 text-sm">
        <p className="text-green-700">
          You're on the list. We'll email you when Thunder Pro is available.
        </p>
        {onClose ? (
          <button
            className="mt-2 rounded bg-gray-200 px-3 py-1"
            onClick={onClose}
          >
            Close
          </button>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-2 p-3 text-sm">
      <label className="block">
        <span className="text-gray-700">Email</span>
        <input
          type="email"
          value={email}
          onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
          className="mt-1 w-full rounded border px-2 py-1"
          placeholder="you@example.com"
        />
      </label>
      {status === "error" ? (
        <p className="text-red-600">{reason}</p>
      ) : null}
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <button
          className="rounded bg-blue-600 px-3 py-1 text-white disabled:opacity-50"
          disabled={status === "sending" || !email}
          onClick={() => void submit()}
        >
          {status === "sending" ? "Submitting…" : "Join waitlist"}
        </button>
        {onClose ? (
          <button
            className="rounded bg-gray-200 px-3 py-1"
            onClick={onClose}
          >
            Cancel
          </button>
        ) : null}
      </div>
      <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">
        Stored locally in your Thunder install. No email is sent from this build.
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}
